### [Exclusion of Certain Insurable Earnings in the Calculation of the Rate of Weekly Benefits of a Self-employed Person]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983054 .Subheading}

**[[24.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.3 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 152.16(1)(b) of the Act, the following insurable earnings are
not taken into account in the calculation of the rate of weekly benefits
of a self-employed person:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} insurable earnings from any employment that they lost
  because of their misconduct or that they voluntarily left without just
  cause within the meaning of paragraphs 29(b.1) and (c) of the Act; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} insurable earnings in the qualifying period earned
  before the beginning of a prior benefit period established under Part
  I of the Act or regulations made under Part VIII of the Act for the
  self-employed person as an insured person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-10, s. 8
:::

### [Week of Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983060 .Subheading}

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of section 145 of the Act, a claimant is considered to
  have been paid a week of benefits when the total of the percentages of
  benefits paid for one or more weeks of unemployment equals 100.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1), the percentage of
  benefits paid for a week shall be obtained by dividing the amount of
  the benefits paid to the claimant for that week by the rate of weekly
  benefits applicable to that week.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-516, s. 1
- SOR/2016-206, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-25-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 25"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Election to Receive Benefits Payable Under Part I or VII.1 of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983066 .Subheading}

**[[25.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25.1 .sectionLabel}** The election referred
to in subsection 152.09(1) of the Act shall be made to the Commission in
writing.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-10, s. 9
:::

### [Claim for Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983070 .Subheading}

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a claim
  for benefits for a week of unemployment in a benefit period shall be
  made by a claimant within three weeks after the week for which
  benefits are claimed.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a claimant has not made a claim for benefits
  for four or more consecutive weeks, the first claim for benefits after
  that period for a week of unemployment shall be made within one week
  after the week for which benefits are claimed.

<!-- -->

- **[[26.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The definitions in this subsection
  apply in this section.

  [[condition of entitlement to benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[condition of entitlement to benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      any requirement, circumstance or condition referred to in
      subsection 49(1) of the Act. ([condition d\'admissibilité au
      bénéfice des prestations]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[period of eligibility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[period of eligibility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the benefits referred to in
        subparagraph (2)(c)(i), one of the following periods, to which
        is added the waiting period referred to in section 13 of the Act
        if that period has not already been served:

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the period referred to in subsection 22(2) of
          the Act and any extension of that period,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the period referred to in subsection 23(2)
          of the Act,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the period referred to in subsection
          23.1(4) of the Act,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the period referred to in subsection 23.2(3)
          of the Act,

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the period referred to in subsection 23.3(3)
          of the Act;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the benefits referred to in
        subparagraph (2)(c)(ii), the period during which the claimant
        attends a course or program, referred to in paragraph 25(1)(a)
        of the Act, that is an apprenticeship course or an
        apprenticeship program; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the benefits referred to in
        subparagraph (2)(c)(iii), the period during which the claimant
        is employed in work-sharing employment referred to in section 24
        of the Act. ([période d'admissibilité]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 26, a claimant is not
  required to make periodic claims for benefits in accordance with that
  section where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant makes an initial claim for benefits or
    a claim in respect of which subsection 26(2) applies;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant\'s period of eligibility ends after
    June 26, 1999;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claimant makes a claim for benefits in respect
    of every week of unemployment in the claimant\'s period of
    eligibility

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for a reason set out in paragraph 12(3)(a), (b),
      (d), (e) or (f) of the Act,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} under section 25 of the Act, to attend an
      apprenticeship course or an apprenticeship program, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} under section 24 of the Act, to receive
      work-sharing benefits, and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the claimant completes a form, provided by the
    Commission, stating that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to the best of the claimant's knowledge at the
      time of completing the form, there are no conditions of
      entitlement to benefits that will not be met for each week in the
      period of eligibility, except in respect of earnings that may be
      deducted under section 19, 22, 23, 23.1, 23.2 or 23.3 of the Act
      during that period,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the claimant will notify the Commission as soon
      as possible if the claimant ceases to meet a condition of
      entitlement to benefits at any time during the period of
      eligibility and failure to meet that condition has the effect of
      reducing or eliminating any benefits for any week in the period of
      eligibility, or if the claimant has earnings referred to in
      subparagraph (i) in respect of that period, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the claimant will notify the Commission at the
      end of the period of eligibility whether or not they have met the
      conditions of entitlement to benefits for each week in the period
      of eligibility and whether or not they have declared all earnings
      that could be deducted under section 19, 22, 23, 23.1, 23.2 or
      23.3 of the Act during that period.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Commission becomes aware that the claimant
  does not meet a condition of entitlement to benefits during the
  claimant\'s period of eligibility, this section shall cease to apply
  in respect of the claimant\'s claim for benefits as of the date on
  which the Commission becomes aware of the claimant\'s failure to meet
  the condition.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/99-241, s. 1
- SOR/2003-393, s. 2
- SOR/2009-96, s. 2
- SOR/2011-229, s. 1(E)
- SOR/2013-102, s. 6
- SOR/2017-226, s. 4
:::

[Previous
Version](section-26.1-20130609.html "Link to previous version of section 26.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Persons with Mental Disabilities, Incapacitated Persons and Deceased Persons]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983100 .Subheading}

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an
  initial claim for benefits or a claim for benefits for a week of
  unemployment is made to the Commission on behalf of a person with
  mental disabilities or an incapacitated person, the Commission shall
  authorize payment of the benefits to any person acting on behalf of
  the person if the person on whose behalf the claim is made meets the
  requirements of Part I, VII.1 or VIII of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an initial claim for benefits or a claim for
  benefits for a week of unemployment is made to the Commission by the
  legal representative of a deceased person, the Commission shall
  authorize payment of the benefits to the legal representative if the
  deceased person, at the time of death, met the requirements of Part I,
  VII.1 or VIII of the Act.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), \"legal
  representative\" means the executor or administrator of the estate of
  the deceased person.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (2), any person entitled
  to succeed to the property of a deceased person may make a claim for
  benefits, in accordance with subsections (5) and (6), which benefits
  are payable to the person where the total value of the estate of the
  deceased person is not sufficient to warrant obtaining

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an intestate succession, letters of
    administration; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a testate succession, probate of the
    will of the deceased person.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The claim for benefits referred to in subsection (4)
  shall be made on a form supplied by the Commission and shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statutory declaration, signed by the applicant,
    stating that the applicant is a person entitled to succeed to the
    property of the deceased person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an undertaking signed by the applicant to repay to
    the Receiver General any moneys paid in error to the applicant.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} When making a claim for benefits referred to in
  subsection (4), the applicant shall also submit

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the will of the deceased person, if one
    exists; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a release of all claims in respect of the amount
    payable, signed by all persons other than the applicant, if any, who
    are entitled to succeed to the property of the deceased person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-10, s. 10
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-27-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 27"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Payments in Advance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983116 .Subheading}

**[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** The Commission may pay
benefits for a week of unemployment to a claimant in advance of the
customary time for paying benefits where

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} that unemployment arises from the occurrence of a
  fire, flood, hurricane, epidemic or other natural disaster or act of
  God at the factory, workshop or other premises or in the area where
  the claimant is employed;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} that week of unemployment is

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the week in which Christmas Day falls or the week
    that precedes the week in which Christmas Day falls, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a week during which the claimant is attending a
    course or program, referred to in paragraph 25(1)(a) of the Act,
    that is an apprenticeship course or apprenticeship program; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} there is, or is anticipated to be, a disruption of
  electrical service, electronic processes or telecommunications that
  will prevent the processing of claims for benefits in the normal
  manner and in a timely fashion.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-16, s. 1
:::

### [Employed Person Working a Full Working Week --- Special Cases]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983125 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/97-31, s. 14

\]
:::

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  claimant is considered to have worked a full working week during each
  week in the two-week period reported on in the declaration filed when
  making a claim for benefits where the claimant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is a railway employee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is remunerated on a mileage basis; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is remunerated in that two-week period at not less
    than twice the maximum weekly insurable earnings.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 31, a claimant who is
  employed in farming or horticulture is considered to have worked a
  full working week during any week in which the claimant works

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} not less than five days; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} not less than 35 hours in total.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where, in any week, a claimant does not work on a
  holiday or, by reason of a holiday, does not work on the working day
  that immediately precedes or immediately follows that holiday, the
  claimant shall be regarded as having worked a full working week if, on
  each of the remaining working days in that week, the claimant works a
  number of hours at least equal to the number that the claimant would
  normally work.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where an insured person is employed under a contract
  of employment under which the usual remuneration is payable in respect
  of a period greater than a week, regardless of the amount of work
  performed in the period, each week that falls wholly in that period is
  a full working week for the person.

### [Self-employed Person Working a Full Working Week]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983138 .Subheading}

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (4),
  where during any week a claimant is self-employed or engaged in the
  operation of a business on the claimant\'s own account or in a
  partnership or co-adventure, or is employed in any other employment in
  which the claimant controls their working hours, the claimant is
  considered to have worked a full working week during that week.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a claimant is employed or engaged in the
  operation of a business as described in subsection (1) to such a minor
  extent that a person would not normally rely on that employment or
  engagement as a principal means of livelihood, the claimant is, in
  respect of that employment or engagement, not regarded as working a
  full working week.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The circumstances to be considered in determining
  whether the claimant\'s employment or engagement in the operation of a
  business is of the minor extent described in subsection (2) are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the time spent;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the nature and amount of the capital and resources
    invested;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the financial success or failure of the employment
    or business;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the continuity of the employment or business;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the nature of the employment or business; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the claimant\'s intention and willingness to seek
    and immediately accept alternate employment.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a claimant is employed in farming and
  subsection (2) does not apply to that employment, the claimant shall
  not be considered to have worked a full working week at any time
  during the period that begins with the week in which October 1st falls
  and ends with the week in which the following March 31 falls, if the
  claimant proves that during that period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant did not work; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant was employed to such a minor extent
    that it would not have prevented the claimant from accepting
    full-time employment.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, [[self-employed
  person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is or was engaged in a business; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is employed but does not have insurable employment
    by reason of paragraph 5(2)(b) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-31, s. 15
- SOR/2003-43, s. 1(F)
- SOR/2010-10, s. 11
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-30-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 30"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Week of Unemployment --- Self-employed Person]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983157 .Subheading}

**[[30.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
Part VII.1 of the Act, a week of unemployment for a self-employed person
is, despite section 30, a week in which the person reduces the time
devoted to their business activities by more than 40% of the normal
level.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-10, s. 12
:::

**[[30.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.2 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 152.03(4) of the Act, a self-employed person is deemed to be
not working if they are not engaged in

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the normal activities of their business; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the normal activities for the continuation of their
  business.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-10, s. 12
:::

### [Employed Persons Working a Full Working Week]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983166 .Subheading}

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A full
  working week of a claimant, other than a claimant referred to in
  section 29 or 30, is the number of hours, days or shifts normally
  worked in a calendar week by persons in the claimant\'s grade, class
  or shift at the factory, workshop or other premises at which the
  claimant is or was employed.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} When the number of hours, days or shifts referred to
  in subsection (1) is the number that is normally worked by persons in
  part-time employment and is less than the number of hours, days or
  shifts normally worked in a calendar week by persons employed in
  full-time employment in the employment that is closest in nature to
  the claimant\'s employment, the claimant is considered to have worked
  a full working week when the claimant has worked the number of hours,
  days or shifts that are normally worked by a person in full-time
  employment.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The full working week of a claimant, other than a
  claimant referred to in section 29 or 30, who is remunerated on a
  piece, mileage or other unit rate is the number of days normally
  worked in a calendar week by persons in the claimant\'s grade, class
  or shift at the factory, workshop or other premises at which the
  claimant is or was employed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-31, s. 16
- SOR/2002-154, s. 3
:::

### [Working Day]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983173 .Subheading}

**[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
sections 18 and 152.19 of the Act, a working day is any day of the week
except Saturday and Sunday.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-10, s. 13
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-32-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 32"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Additional Conditions and Terms in Relation to Teachers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983177 .Subheading}

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in this section.

  [[non-teaching period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-teaching period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the period that
      occurs annually at regular or irregular intervals during which no
      work is performed by a significant number of people employed in
      teaching. ([période de congé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[teaching]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[teaching]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the occupation of teaching
      in a pre-elementary, an elementary or a secondary school,
      including a technical or vocational
      school. ([enseignement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who was employed in teaching for any part
  of the claimant's qualifying period is not entitled to receive
  benefits, other than those payable under section 22, 23, 23.1, 23.2 or
  23.3 of the Act, for any week of unemployment that falls in any
  non-teaching period of the claimant unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant\'s contract of employment for teaching
    has terminated;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant\'s employment in teaching was on a
    casual or substitute basis; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claimant qualifies to receive benefits in
    respect of employment in an occupation other than teaching.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a claimant who was employed in teaching for any
  part of the claimant\'s qualifying period qualifies to receive
  benefits in respect of employment in an occupation other than
  teaching, the amount of benefits payable for a week of unemployment
  that falls within any non-teaching period of the claimant shall be
  limited to the amount that is payable in respect of the employment in
  that other occupation.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-31, s. 17
- SOR/2003-393, s. 3
- SOR/2013-102, s. 7
- SOR/2017-226, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-33-20130609.html "Link to previous version of section 33"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Family Supplement --- Rate Increase]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983189 .Subheading}

- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this section, [[cohabitating spouse or common-law
  partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 122.6
  of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, a Canada child
  benefit is deemed to be an overpayment under Subdivision a.1 of
  Division E of Part I of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

- [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/99-290, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant establishes, in the manner directed by
  the Commission under section 16 or 152.17 of the Act, that the
  claimant or the claimant's cohabiting spouse or common-law partner is
  in receipt of a Canada child benefit for the month preceding the
  Sunday of the week in respect of which the claimant makes a claim for
  benefits, the claimant's rate of weekly benefits for that week shall
  be increased by the amount of a family supplement determined in
  accordance with this section.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The claimant's rate of weekly benefits for a week
  shall not be increased under this section if benefits are also payable
  to the claimant's cohabiting spouse or common law partner for that
  week at a rate increased under this section.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The family supplement is the amount determined in
  accordance with the following table, taking into account the family
  income and the number and ages of the children for which the claimant
  or the claimant's cohabiting spouse or common-law partner receives a
  Canada child benefit:

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The amount of the family supplement shall not exceed
  the following percentage of the claimant's weekly insurable earnings:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for benefit periods established in 1997, 10%;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for benefit periods established in 1998, 15%;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for benefit periods established in 1999, 20%; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for benefit periods established in 2000 or later,
    25%.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Where the application of subsection (5) in respect of
  benefit periods established before August 1, 1999 results in a
  reduction of the amount of the family supplement payable to the
  claimant for a week of unemployment falling on or after August 1,
  1999, the amount of the family supplement so calculated will be
  increased for each child for whom the claimant is entitled to receive
  a Canada child benefit by an additional amount determined in
  accordance with the following table, taking into account the family
  income and the number and ages of the children, for the duration of
  the benefit period:

### [Determination of Earnings for Benefit Purposes]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983217 .Subheading}

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in this section.

  [[employment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[employment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any employment, whether insurable, not
        insurable or excluded employment, under any express or implied
        contract of service or other contract of employment,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} whether or not services are or will be
          provided by a claimant to any other person, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} whether or not income received by the
          claimant is from a person other than the person to whom
          services are or will be provided;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any self-employment, whether on the claimant\'s
        own account or in partnership or co-adventure; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the tenure of an office as defined in
        subsection 2(1) of the Canada Pension
        Plan. ([emploi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any pecuniary or
      non-pecuniary income that is or will be received by a claimant
      from an employer or any other person, including a trustee in
      bankruptcy. ([revenu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pension]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pension]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a retirement pension

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} arising out of employment or out of service in
        any armed forces or in a police force;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} under the Canada Pension Plan; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} under a provincial pension
        plan. ([pension]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[self-employed person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[self-employed person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning
      as in subsection 30(5). ([travailleur
      indépendant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of this section, the
  earnings to be taken into account for the purpose of determining
  whether an interruption of earnings under section 14 has occurred and
  the amount to be deducted from benefits payable under section 19,
  subsection 21(3), 22(5), 152.03(3) or 152.04(4) or section 152.18 of
  the Act, and to be taken into account for the purposes of sections 45
  and 46 of the Act, are the entire income of a claimant arising out of
  any employment, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} amounts payable to a claimant in respect of wages,
    benefits or other remuneration from the proceeds realized from the
    property of a bankrupt employer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} workers\' compensation payments received or to be
    received by a claimant, other than a lump sum or pension paid in
    full and final settlement of a claim made for workers\' compensation
    payments;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} payments a claimant has received or, on
    application, is entitled to receive under

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a group wage-loss indemnity plan,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a paid sick, maternity or adoption leave plan,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a leave plan providing payment in respect of
      the care of a child or children referred to in subsection 23(1) or
      152.05(1) of the Act,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a leave plan providing payment in respect of the
      care or support of a family member referred to in subsection
      23.1(2) or 152.06(1) of the Act,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a leave plan providing payment in respect of the
      care or support of a critically ill child, or

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a leave plan providing payment in respect of the
      care or support of a critically ill adult;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} notwithstanding paragraph (7)(b) but subject to
    subsections (3) and (3.1), the payments a claimant has received or,
    on application, is entitled to receive from a motor vehicle accident
    insurance plan provided under a provincial law in respect of the
    actual or presumed loss of income from employment due to injury, if
    the benefits paid or payable under the Act are not taken into
    account in determining the amount that the claimant receives or is
    entitled to receive from the plan;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the moneys paid or payable to a claimant on a
    periodic basis or in a lump sum on account of or in lieu of a
    pension; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} where the benefits paid or payable under the Act
    are not taken into account in determining the amount that a claimant
    receives or is entitled to receive pursuant to a provincial law in
    respect of an actual or presumed loss of income from employment, the
    indemnity payments the claimant has received or, on application, is
    entitled to receive pursuant to that provincial law by reason of the
    fact that the claimant has ceased to work for the reason that
    continuation of work entailed physical dangers for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the claimant,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the claimant\'s unborn child, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the child the claimant is breast-feeding.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where, subsequent to the week in which an injury
  referred to in paragraph (2)(d) occurs, a claimant has accumulated the
  number of hours of insurable employment required by section 7 or 7.1
  of the Act, the payments referred to in that paragraph shall not be
  taken into account as earnings.

- [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} If a self-employed person has sustained an injury
  referred to in paragraph (2)(d) before the beginning of the period
  referred to in section 152.08 of the Act, the payments referred to in
  that paragraph shall not be taken into account as earnings.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (2), the payments a
  claimant has received or, on application, is entitled to receive under
  a group sickness or disability wage-loss indemnity plan or a workers\'
  compensation plan, or as an indemnity described in paragraph (2)(f),
  are not earnings to be taken into account for the purpose of
  subsection 14(2).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (2), the moneys referred
  to in paragraph (2)(e) are not earnings to be taken into account for
  the purposes of section 14.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (2), the earnings referred
  to in subsection 36(9) and allowances that would not be deducted from
  benefits by virtue of subsection 16(1) are not earnings to be taken
  into account for the purposes of section 14.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} That portion of the income of a claimant that is
  derived from any of the following sources does not constitute earnings
  for the purposes referred to in subsection (2):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} disability pension or a lump sum or pension paid in
    full and final settlement of a claim made for workers\' compensation
    payments;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} payments under a sickness or disability wage-loss
    indemnity plan that is not a group plan;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} relief grants in cash or in kind;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} retroactive increases in wages or salary;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the moneys referred to in paragraph (2)(e) if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a self-employed person, the moneys
      became payable before the beginning of the period referred to in
      section 152.08 of the Act, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of other claimants, the number of
      hours of insurable employment required by section 7 or 7.1 of the
      Act for the establishment of their benefit period was accumulated
      after the date on which those moneys became payable and during the
      period in respect of which they received those moneys; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} employment income excluded as income pursuant to
    subsection 6(16) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2021, c. 23, s. 347\]]{.Repealed}

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraphs (2)(c) and (7)(b), a
  sickness or disability wage-loss indemnity plan is not a group plan if
  it is a plan that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is not related to a group of persons who are all
    employed by the same employer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not financed in whole or in part by an employer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is voluntarily purchased by the person
    participating in the plan;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is completely portable;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} provides constant benefits while permitting
    deductions for income from other sources, where applicable; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} has rates of premium that do not depend on the
    experience of a group referred to in paragraph (a).

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (8),
  [[portable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a plan referred to in
  that subsection, means that the benefits to which an employee covered
  by the plan is entitled and the rate of premium that the employee is
  required to pay while employed by an employer will remain equivalent
  if the employee becomes employed by any other employer within the same
  occupation.

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2),
  [[income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claimant who is not self-employed,
    that amount of the claimant\'s income remaining after deducting

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} expenses incurred by the claimant for the direct
      purpose of earning that income, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the value of any consideration supplied by the
      claimant; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claimant who is self-employed in
    farming, the gross income from that self-employment, including any
    farming subsidies the claimant receives under any federal or
    provincial program, remaining after deducting the operating
    expenses, other than capital expenditures, incurred in that
    self-employment;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claimant who is self-employed in
    employment other than farming, the amount of the gross income from
    that employment remaining after deducting the operating expenses,
    other than capital expenditures, incurred therein; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any claimant, the value of board,
    living quarters and other benefits received by the claimant from or
    on behalf of the claimant\'s employer in respect of the claimant\'s
    employment.

- [(11)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (12), the value of the
  benefits referred to in paragraph (10)(d) shall be the amount fixed by
  agreement between the claimant and the claimant\'s employer and shall
  be an amount that is reasonable in the circumstances.

- [(12)]{.lawlabel} Where the claimant and the employer do not agree on
  the value of the benefits referred to in paragraph (10)(d), or where
  the value fixed for those benefits by agreement between the claimant
  and the claimant\'s employer is not reasonable in the circumstances,
  the value shall be determined by the Commission based on the monetary
  value of the benefits.

- [(13)]{.lawlabel} The value of living quarters referred to in
  paragraph (10)(d) includes the value of any heat, light, telephone or
  other benefits included with the living quarters.

- [(14)]{.lawlabel} Where the value of living quarters is determined by
  the Commission, it shall be computed on the rental value of similar
  living quarters in the same vicinity or district.

- [(15)]{.lawlabel} Where the remuneration of a claimant is not
  pecuniary or is only partly pecuniary and all or part of the
  non-pecuniary remuneration consists of any consideration other than
  living quarters and board furnished by the employer, the value of that
  consideration shall be included in determining the claimant\'s income.

- [(16)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, living quarters
  means rooms or any other living accommodation.

### [Allocation of Earnings for Benefit Purposes]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983285 .Subheading}

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  earnings of a claimant as determined under section 35 shall be
  allocated to weeks in the manner described in this section and, for
  the purposes referred to in subsection 35(2), shall be the earnings of
  the claimant for those weeks.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, the earnings of a
  claimant shall not be allocated to weeks during which they did not
  constitute earnings or were not taken into account as earnings under
  section 35.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the period for which earnings of a claimant are
  payable does not coincide with a week, the earnings shall be allocated
  to any week that is wholly or partly in the period in the proportion
  that the number of days worked in the week bears to the number of days
  worked in the period.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Earnings that are payable to a claimant under a
  contract of employment for the performance of services shall be
  allocated to the period in which the services were performed.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Earnings that are payable to a claimant under a
  contract of employment without the performance of services or payable
  by an employer to a claimant in consideration of the claimant
  returning to or beginning work shall be allocated to the period for
  which they are payable.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The earnings of a claimant who is self-employed, or
  the earnings of a claimant that are from participation in profits or
  commissions, that arise from the performance of services shall be
  allocated to the weeks in which those services are performed.

- [(6.1)]{.lawlabel} The earnings of a claimant who is self-employed, or
  the earnings of a claimant that are from participation in profits or
  commissions, that arise from a transaction shall be allocated

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the aggregate amount of earnings that arise from
    a transaction occurring in a week is greater than the maximum yearly
    insurable earnings referred to in section 4 of the Act divided by
    52, to the weeks in which the work that gave rise to the transaction
    was performed, in a manner that is proportional to the amount of
    work that was performed during each of those weeks or, if no such
    work was performed, to the week in which the transaction occurred;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the aggregate amount of earnings that arise from
    a transaction occurring in a week is less than or equal to the
    maximum yearly insurable earnings referred to in section 4 of the
    Act divided by 52, to the week in which the transaction occurred or,
    if the claimant demonstrates that the work that gave rise to the
    transaction occurred in more than one week, to the weeks in which
    the earnings were earned, in a manner that is proportional to the
    amount of work that was performed during each of those weeks.

- [(6.2)]{.lawlabel} The earnings of a claimant who is self-employed, or
  the earnings of a claimant that are from participation in profits or
  commissions, that do not arise from the performance of services or
  from a transaction shall be allocated equally to each week falling
  within the period in which the earnings were earned.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The earnings of a claimant who is self-employed in
  farming shall be allocated

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they arose from a transaction, in accordance
    with subsection (6.1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they were received in the form of a subsidy, to
    the week in which the subsidy was paid.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} Where vacation pay is paid or payable to a claimant
  for a reason other than a lay-off or separation from an employment, it
  shall be allocated as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the vacation pay is paid or payable for a
    specific vacation period or periods, it shall be allocated

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to a number of weeks that begins with the first
      week and ends not later than the last week of the vacation period
      or periods, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in such a manner that the total earnings of the
      claimant from that employment are, in each consecutive week, equal
      to the claimant's normal weekly earnings from that employment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the vacation pay shall, when
    paid, be allocated

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to a number of weeks that begins with the first
      week for which it is payable, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in such a manner that, for each week except the
      last, the amount allocated under this subsection is equal to the
      claimant's normal weekly earnings from that employment.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (10) to (11), all earnings
  paid or payable to a claimant by reason of a lay-off or separation
  from an employment shall, regardless of the period in respect of which
  the earnings are purported to be paid or payable, be allocated to a
  number of weeks that begins with the week of the lay-off or separation
  in such a manner that the total earnings of the claimant from that
  employment are, in each consecutive week except the last, equal to the
  claimant's normal weekly earnings from that employment.

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (11), where earnings are paid
  or payable to a claimant by reason of a lay-off or separation from an
  employment subsequent to an allocation under subsection (9) in respect
  of that lay-off or separation, the subsequent earnings shall be added
  to the earnings that were allocated and, regardless of the period in
  respect of which the subsequent earnings are purported to be paid or
  payable, a revised allocation shall be made in accordance with
  subsection (9) on the basis of that total.

- [(10.1)]{.lawlabel} The allocation of the earnings paid or payable to
  a claimant by reason of a lay-off or separation from an employment
  made in accordance with subsection (9) does not apply if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant's benefit period begins in the period
    beginning on January 25, 2009 and ending on May 29, 2010;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant contributed at least 30% of the
    maximum annual employee's premium in at least seven of the 10 years
    before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Commission paid the claimant less than 36 weeks
    of regular benefits in the 260 weeks before the beginning of the
    claimant's benefit period; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} during the period in which the earnings paid or
    payable by reason of the claimant's lay-off or separation from an
    employment are allocated in accordance with subsection (9) or, if
    the earnings are allocated to five weeks or less, during that period
    of allocation or within six weeks following the notification of the
    allocation, the claimant is referred by the Commission, or an
    authority that the Commission designates, under paragraph 25(1)(a)
    of the Act, to a course or program of instruction or training

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is full-time,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that has a duration of at least 10 weeks or that
      costs at least \$5,000 or 80% of the earnings paid or payable by
      reason of the claimant's lay-off or separation from employment,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for which the claimant assumes the entire cost,
      and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} that begins during one of the 52 weeks following
      the beginning of the claimant's benefit period.

- [(10.2)]{.lawlabel} If any of the conditions under which the
  Commission may terminate the claimant's referral under paragraph
  27(1.1)(b) of the Act exists, the earnings paid or payable to the
  claimant by reason of a lay-off or separation from an employment shall
  be re-allocated under subsection (9).

- [(11)]{.lawlabel} Where earnings are paid or payable in respect of an
  employment pursuant to a labour arbitration award or the judgment of a
  tribunal, or as a settlement of an issue that might otherwise have
  been determined by a labour arbitration award or the judgment of a
  tribunal, and the earnings are awarded in respect of specific weeks as
  a result of a finding or admission that disciplinary action was
  warranted, the earnings shall be allocated to a number of consecutive
  weeks, beginning with the first week in respect of which the earnings
  are awarded, in such a manner that the total earnings of the claimant
  from that employment are, in each week except the last week, equal to
  the claimant's normal weekly earnings from that employment.

- [(12)]{.lawlabel} The following payments shall be allocated to the
  weeks in respect of which the payments are paid or payable:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} payments in respect of sick leave, maternity leave
    or adoption leave or leave for the care of a child or children
    referred to in subsection 23(1) or 152.05(1) of the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} payments under a group sickness or disability
    wage-loss indemnity plan;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} payments referred to in paragraphs 35(2)(d) and
    (f);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} workers' compensation payments, other than a lump
    sum or pension paid in full and final settlement of a claim made for
    workers' compensation payments;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} payments in respect of the care or support of a
    family member referred to in subsection 23.1(2) or 152.06(1) of the
    Act;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} payments in respect of the care or support of a
    critically ill child; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} payments in respect of the care or support of a
    critically ill adult.

- [(13)]{.lawlabel} A payment paid or payable to a claimant in respect
  of a holiday or non-working day that is observed as such by law,
  custom or agreement, or a holiday or non-working day immediately
  preceding or following a holiday or non-working day that occurs at the
  establishment of the employer or former employer from whom the
  claimant receives that payment, shall be allocated to the week in
  which that day occurs.

- [(14)]{.lawlabel} The moneys referred to in paragraph 35(2)(e) that
  are paid or payable to a claimant on a periodic basis shall be
  allocated to the period for which they are paid or payable.

- [(15)]{.lawlabel} The moneys referred to in paragraph 35(2)(e) that
  are paid or payable to a claimant in a lump sum shall be allocated
  beginning with the first week that those moneys are paid or payable to
  the claimant in such a manner that those moneys are equal in each week
  to the weekly amount, calculated in accordance with subsection (17),
  to which the claimant would have been entitled if the lump sum payment
  had been paid as an annuity.

- [(16)]{.lawlabel} The moneys allocated in accordance with
  subsection (14) or (15) shall not be taken into account in the
  allocation of other earnings under this section.

- [(17)]{.lawlabel} The weekly amount shall be calculated in accordance
  with the following formula, according to the claimant's age on the day
  on which the lump sum payment is paid or payable:

  ::: {#983333 .Subsection}
  A / B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the lump sum payment; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the estimated actuarial present value[[Footnote
      ]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_star_e){#fn_star_e-ID0EBAACEA} of \$1
      payable at the beginning of every week starting from the day on
      which the lump sum payment is paid or payable and payable for the
      claimant's lifetime, as calculated each year in accordance with
      the following formula and effective on January 1 of the year
      following its calculation:
  :::

  ::: {#983337 .Subsection}
  B = \[Σ~t\ =\ 0~ to infinity of (~t~P~x~/ (1+ i)^t^) -- 0.5\] × 52

  where

  [~t~P~x~]{.dfn}
  :   is the probability that the claimant will survive for "t" years
      from the claimant's age "x" using the latest Canadian mortality
      rates used in the valuation of the Canada Pension Plan prorated in
      equal parts between males and females,

  [i]{.dfn}
  :   is the annualized long-term Government of Canada benchmark bond
      yields averaged over the 12-month period beginning on the
      September 1 and ending on the August 30 before the January 1 on
      which the estimated actuarial present values are effective,
      expressed as a percentage and rounded to the nearest one tenth of
      a percentage, and

  [t]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of years that the claimant survives according to the
      claimant's age for which the probablity of survival is estimated
      by ~t~P~x~.
  :::

  - ::: Footnote
    [[Return to footnote
    ]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_star_e-ID0EBAACEA){#fn_star_e}Note: The
    estimated actuarial present values are published annually on the
    Service Canada website.
    :::

- [(18)]{.lawlabel} Earnings that are payable to a claimant under a
  government program intended to encourage re-employment and that are
  payable to the claimant as a supplement to earnings arising from a
  contract of employment shall be allocated to the period for which they
  are payable.

- [(19)]{.lawlabel} Where a claimant has earnings to which none of
  subsections (1) to (18) apply, those earnings shall be allocated

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they arise from the performance of services, to
    the period in which the services are performed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they arise from a transaction, to the week in
    which the transaction occurs.

- [(20)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, a fraction of a
  dollar that is equal to or greater than one half shall be taken as a
  dollar and a fraction that is less than one half shall be disregarded.

### [Supplemental Unemployment Benefit Plans]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983349 .Subheading}

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of
  this section, payments received by a claimant as an insured person
  under a supplemental unemployment benefit plan are not earnings for
  the purposes of section 19, subsection 21(3), section 45 or 46,
  subsection 152.03(3) or section 152.18 of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1), a supplemental
  unemployment benefit plan is a plan that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} identifies the group or groups of employees covered
    by the plan;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} covers any period of unemployment by reason of a
    temporary stoppage of work, training, illness, injury, quarantine or
    any combination of such reasons;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} requires employees to apply for and be in receipt
    of benefits in order to receive payments under the plan but may
    provide for payments to an employee who is not in receipt of
    benefits for the reason that the employee

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is serving the waiting period,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has insufficient hours of insurable employment
      to qualify for benefits, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} has received all of the benefits to which the
      employee is entitled;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} requires that the combined weekly payments received
    from the plan and the portion of the weekly benefit rate from that
    employment do not exceed 95 per cent of the employee\'s normal
    weekly earnings from that employment;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} requires that payments under the plan be financed
    by the employer and that the employer keep separate accounts for
    those payments;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} requires that, on termination of the plan, all
    remaining assets revert to the employer or be used for payments
    under the plan or for administrative costs of the plan;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} requires that the plan be submitted to the
    Commission prior to its effective date and that written notice of
    any change to the plan be given to the Commission within 30 days
    after the effective date of the change;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} provides that the employees have no vested right to
    payments under the plan, except to payments during a period of
    unemployment specified in the plan; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} provides that payments in respect of guaranteed
    annual remuneration or in respect of deferred remuneration or
    severance pay benefits are not reduced or increased by payments
    received under the plan.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2016-314, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-154, s. 6
- SOR/2002-274, s. 1
- SOR/2010-10, s. 17
- SOR/2016-314, s. 1
:::

### [Maternity Leave, Leave for the Care of a Child and Compassionate Care Leave Plans]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983368 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2002-274, s. 2
- SOR/2003-393, s. 6

\]
:::

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following portion of any payments that are paid to a claimant as an
  insured person because of pregnancy, for the care of a child or
  children referred to in subsection 23(1) or 152.05(1) of the Act or
  for the care or support of a family member referred to in subsection
  23.1(2) or 152.06(1) of the Act, of a critically ill child or of a
  critically ill adult, or because of any combination of those reasons,
  is excluded as earnings for the purposes of section 35, namely, the
  portion that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when combined with the portion of the claimant\'s
    weekly benefit rate from that employment, does not exceed that
    claimant\'s normal weekly earnings from that employment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} does not reduce the claimant\'s accumulated sick
    leave or vacation leave credits, severance pay or any other
    accumulated credits from that claimant\'s employment.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2016-314, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} However, the portion of any payments referred to in
  subsection (1) that are paid to a claimant in respect of a week during
  which benefits are payable to them by reason of illness, injury or
  quarantine is considered earnings for the purposes of section 35.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-274, s. 3
- SOR/2003-393, s. 7
- SOR/2010-10, s. 18
- SOR/2013-26, s. 1
- SOR/2013-102, s. 10
- SOR/2016-314, s. 2
- SOR/2017-226, s. 8
:::

### [Earnings in the Waiting Period]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983379 .Subheading}

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  claimant has earnings in respect of a period that falls in the
  claimant's waiting period, an amount equal to those earnings or, if
  paragraph 19(3)(a) or 152.18(3)(a) of the Act applies in respect of
  those earnings, the amount required by that paragraph to be deducted,
  shall be deducted from the benefits payable for the first three weeks
  for which benefits are otherwise payable.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount to be deducted under
  subsection (1) in respect of a claimant's earnings in their waiting
  period is an amount equal to their rate of weekly benefits.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of calculating the earnings of a
  claimant during the waiting period, no account shall be taken of
  amounts paid or payable

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} under a wage-loss indemnity plan by reason of
    illness, injury or quarantine, pregnancy, the care of a child or
    children referred to in subsection 23(1) or 152.05(1) of the Act or
    the care or support of a family member referred to in subsection
    23.1(2) or 152.06(1) of the Act, of a critically ill child or of a
    critically ill adult, or under a workers' compensation plan; or

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} as an indemnity described in paragraph 35(2)(f);
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by an employer in respect of sick leave, maternity
    leave or adoption leave, leave for the care of a child or children
    referred to in subsection 23(1) or 152.05(1) of the Act or leave for
    the care or support of a family member referred to in subsection
    23.1(2) or 152.06(1) of the Act, of a critically ill child or of a
    critically ill adult.

### [Waiving of the Waiting Period for Apprentices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983389 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2021-3, s. 3(F)

\]
:::

**[[39.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39.1 .sectionLabel}** The waiting period
shall be waived if the following conditions are met:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant is attending a course that is a required
  part of an apprenticeship program and to which they are referred
  pursuant to paragraph 25(1)(a) of the Act;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant has ceased working for the reason
  described in paragraph (a); and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claimant has, after the coming into force of this
  section, served a waiting period in respect of a course that is a
  required part of the same apprenticeship program.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-280, s. 1
:::

### [Sickness]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983396 .Subheading}

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  information and evidence to be provided to the Commission by a
  claimant in order to prove inability to work because of illness,
  injury or quarantine under paragraph 18(1)(b) or subsection 152.03(1)
  of the Act, is a medical certificate completed by a medical doctor or
  other medical professional attesting to the claimant's inability to
  work and stating the probable duration of the illness, injury or
  quarantine.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2020-44, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding that a certificate is provided by a
  claimant pursuant to subsection (1), the Commission may require a
  claimant to undergo a medical examination at such time and place as it
  may reasonably direct for the purpose of determining the nature of the
  illness, injury or quarantine, the physical or mental condition of the
  claimant, the probable duration of the inability to work and any other
  circumstances relating to that inability.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A medical examination referred to in subsection (2)
  shall be made at the expense of the Commission and a claimant who
  undergoes such an examination shall be reimbursed for their reasonable
  travel and other expenses.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraphs 8(2)(a) and 18(1)(b)
  and subsections 28(7) and 152.03(1) of the Act, illness, injury or
  quarantine is any illness, injury or quarantine that renders a
  claimant incapable of performing the duties of their regular or usual
  employment or of other suitable employment.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A pregnancy that is terminated within the first 19
  weeks is an illness for the purposes of paragraph 18(1)(b) and
  subsection 152.03(1) of the Act.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may waive the waiting period in
  respect of the benefit period of a claimant if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant qualifies to receive benefits in that
    benefit period by reason of an interruption of earnings as described
    in subsection 14(2) or section 14.01; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after the claimant ceased to work, allowances,
    payments or other moneys are payable to the claimant by the
    claimant\'s employer or former employer as sick leave pay.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2020-44, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2020-44, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2020-44, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote

- SOR/2010-10, s. 20
- SOR/2013-102, s. 20

### [Pregnancy]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983418 .Subheading}

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of sections 22 and 152.04 of the Act, the information and
  evidence to be provided to the Commission by a claimant to prove
  pregnancy and the expected date of confinement is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement signed by the claimant attesting to her
    pregnancy and declaring her expected date of confinement; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} information regarding the actual date of the birth
    of the child, provided by the claimant to the Commission in person,
    by mail or by telephone as soon as is reasonably possible after the
    birth of the child.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph 8(2)(a) of the Act, a
  pregnancy is any pregnancy that renders a claimant incapable of
  performing the duties of her regular or usual employment or of other
  suitable employment.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-10, s. 21
:::

### [Parental, Compassionate Care, Critically Ill Child and Critically Ill Adult Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983428 .Subheading}

#### [Medical Certificate Outside Canada]{.HTitleText3} {#h-983429 .Subheading}

**[[41.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41.2 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsections 23.1(3), 23.2(2), 23.3(2), 152.06(2), 152.061(2) and
152.062(2) of the Act, the medical certificate referred to in
subsections 23.1(2), 23.2(1), 23.3(1), 152.06(1), 152.061(1) or
152.062(1) of the Act may be issued by an individual authorized by the
appropriate governmental authority of a country other than Canada to
perform the work of a medical doctor or of a nurse practitioner and who
has professional qualifications that are substantially similar to those
of a medical doctor or of a nurse practitioner practising in Canada, if
the person to whom the certificate refers is in that country.

::: HistoricalNote

- SOR/2010-10, s. 23
- SOR/2011-229, s. 2
- SOR/2017-226, s. 10
:::

#### [Division of Benefits]{.HTitleText3} {#h-983436 .Subheading}

**[[41.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41.6 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsections 23(4), 23.1(9), 23.2(8), 23.3(6), 152.05(12), 152.06(7),
152.061(8) and 152.062(6) of the Act and subject to subsections 76.21(2)
and 76.42(2), if the claimants cannot agree on a division of the
remaining weeks of unpaid benefits, those weeks shall be divided as
follows:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the number of weeks of unpaid benefits equals the
  number of claimants, each claimant will be paid a week of benefits;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the number of weeks of unpaid benefits is more
  than the number of claimants, a week of benefits will be paid to
  claimants in turn starting with the first claimant to make a claim
  until all the weeks have been exhausted; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the number of weeks of unpaid benefits is less
  than the number of claimants, a week of benefits will be paid to
  claimants in turn starting with the first claimant to make a claim
  until all the weeks have been exhausted.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-102, s. 12
- SOR/2017-226, s. 10
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41.6-20130609.html "Link to previous version of section 41.6"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Work-Sharing Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983443 .Subheading}

**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** Work-sharing benefits are
payable to a claimant who is employed in work-sharing employment for
each week of unemployment that falls in a benefit period established for
the claimant, and subject to sections 43 to 49, the Act and any
regulations made under the Act apply to the claimant, with such
modifications as the circumstances require.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-43, s. 2
:::

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** An interruption of
earnings occurs, in respect of a person employed in work-sharing
employment, at the beginning of the week in which there is a reduction
of at least 10 per cent in the person\'s normal weekly earnings.

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** A claimant is not
entitled to work-sharing benefits for any week for which the claimant
claims benefits under section 12 of the Act.

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** Where a benefit period
has been established in respect of a claimaint and for any week during
that benefit period the claimant is employed in work-sharing employment,
the benefit period shall be extended by the total of those weeks and
subsections 10(12) to (15) of the Act apply, with such modifications as
the circumstances require.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-157, s. 2
:::

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** If a claimant becomes
employed in work-sharing employment and the waiting period has not been
served as required by section 13 of the Act or earnings have not been
deducted as required by subsection 19(1) of the Act, the serving of the
period or the deduction of the earnings shall be deferred until that
employment has terminated.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-314, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-46-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 46"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Earnings received for any week by a
  claimant from work-sharing employment shall not be deducted from the
  work-sharing benefits payable pursuant to section 24 of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant receives earnings for any week other
  than by reason of work-sharing employment, the amount determined under
  subsection 19(2) of the Act shall be deducted from the work-sharing
  benefits payable to the claimant for that week.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-175, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-47-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 47"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** The rate of weekly
benefits payable to a claimant employed under a work-sharing agreement
approved by the Commission for the purposes of section 24 of the Act is
an amount that bears the same ratio to the claimant\'s rate of weekly
benefits determined pursuant to section 14 of the Act that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of hours, days or shifts that the claimant
  did not work because of the work-sharing agreement

bears to

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of hours, days or shifts that the claimant
  would have worked for the employer according to the claimant\'s usual
  work schedule.

**[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** Work-sharing benefits
shall not be taken into account for the purpose of paragraph 10(8)(a) of
the Act or for determining the benefits payable under sections 22 and 23
of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-157, s. 3
:::

### [Employment Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983467 .Subheading}

**[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** The following employment
benefits are prescribed for the purposes of subparagraph 25(1)(b)(i) of
the Act:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Job Creation Partnerships employment benefit
  established by the Commission under section 59 of the Act; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Self-employment employment benefit established by
  the Commission under section 59 of the Act.

### [Work-force Reduction Process]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983471 .Subheading}

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the Act and these
  Regulations, but notwithstanding section 30 of the Act, a claimant who
  has left employment in accordance with an employer work-force
  reduction process that preserves the employment of co-workers may be
  paid benefits where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant accepted an offer to leave that
    employment voluntarily; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the employer has confirmed that the claimant\'s
    leaving resulted in the actual preservation of the employment of a
    co-worker whose employment would otherwise have been terminated in
    the course of the work-force reduction process.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), an employer
  work-force reduction process is a process

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is initiated by the employer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that has as its objective a permanent reduction in
    the overall number of employees;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that offers employees the option to leave
    employment voluntarily; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the elements of which, including the elements
    described in paragraphs (a) to (c), are documented by the employer.

### [Prescribed Circumstances for the Purposes of Subparagraph 29(c)(xiv) of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983481 .Subheading}

**[[51.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subparagraph 29(c)(xiv) of the Act, other reasonable circumstances
include

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} circumstances in which a claimant has an obligation
  to accompany to another residence a person with whom the claimant has
  been cohabiting in a conjugal relationship for a period of less than
  one year and where

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the claimant or that person has had a child during
    that period or has adopted a child during that period,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the claimant or that person is expecting the birth
    of a child, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a child has been placed with the claimant or that
    person during that period for the purpose of adoption; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} circumstances in which a claimant has an obligation
  to care for a member of their immediate family within the meaning of
  subsection 55(2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-290, s. 2
:::

### [Loss of Part-time Employment Due to Work Stoppage]{.HTitleText2} {#h-983490 .Subheading}

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), where a
  claimant loses a part-time employment or is unable to resume a
  part-time employment for any reason mentioned in subsection 36(1) of
  the Act, the maximum number of days of disentitlement of the claimant
  is five days per week ending on the occurrence, in respect of the
  part-time employment, of an event referred to in paragraph 36(1)(a)
  or (b) of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a claimant loses a part-time employment or is
  unable to resume a part-time employment for any reason mentioned in
  subsection 36(1) of the Act, the number of days of disentitlement of
  the claimant in a week is, for the percentage that is set out in
  column I of the table to this subsection and that is the ratio between
  the claimant's average weekly insurable earnings in that part-time
  employment and the weekly insurable earnings as determined under
  section 14 of the Act, the corresponding number of days of
  disentitlement set out in column II of that table, ending on the
  occurrence, in respect of the part-time employment, of an event
  referred to in paragraph 36(1)(a) or (b) of the Act.
