#### [Week Not Counted to Extend Qualifying Period]{.HTitleText3} {#h-983941 .Subheading}

**[[76.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.13 .sectionLabel}** The reference to "a
week during which the person was in receipt of benefits" in subsection
8(5) of the Act shall be read as including a week for which the person
was paid provincial benefits within the meaning of this Part or Part
III.2.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
- SOR/2010-301, s. 7
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76.13-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 76.13"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Extension of the Benefit Period]{.HTitleText3} {#h-983945 .Subheading}

**[[76.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.14 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
extending a claimant's benefit period under subsection 10(13) of the
Act,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a reference in that subsection to benefits paid for
  one of the reasons mentioned in paragraphs 12(3)(a) and (b) of the Act
  is considered to include a reference to provincial benefits paid for
  the same reasons; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the reference in that subsection to "the benefit
  period is extended so that those benefits may be paid up to that
  maximum total number of weeks" shall be read as a reference to "the
  benefit period is extended so that those benefits paid for one of the
  reasons mentioned in paragraphs 12(3)(c) to (f) may be paid up to the
  applicable maximum number of weeks established for those reasons".

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
- SOR/2013-102, s. 18
- SOR/2017-226, s. 16
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76.14-20130609.html "Link to previous version of section 76.14"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[76.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.15 .sectionLabel}** If the child or
children of a claimant who has received or is entitled to receive
provincial benefits are hospitalized during the period referred to in
subsection 23(2) of the Act, the claimant's benefit period is considered
to be extended under subsection 10(12) of the Act by the number of weeks
during which the child or children are hospitalized.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
:::

#### [Reduction of Benefits]{.HTitleText3} {#h-983954 .Subheading}

**[[76.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.16 .sectionLabel}** The benefits
payable under section 22 or 23 of the Act to a claimant in respect of
any week in respect of which the claimant has received or is entitled to
receive provincial benefits shall be reduced as provided by section
76.17.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
:::

**[[76.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.17 .sectionLabel}** An amount equal to
the amount of the provincial benefits that a claimant has received or is
entitled to receive in respect of any week under a provincial plan shall
be deducted from the amount of benefits payable under section 22 or 23
of the Act to the claimant in respect of that same week, in addition to
any deductions referred to in section 19 and subsections 22(5) and
23(3.5) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
:::

#### [No Double Counting of Weeks]{.HTitleText3} {#h-983961 .Subheading}

**[[76.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.18 .sectionLabel}** If a week of
provincial benefits has been taken into account for the purpose of any
of sections 76.11 to 76.14 or 76.19, a week of benefits paid under
section 22 or 23 of the Act shall not be taken into account for that
same purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
- SOR/2016-206, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76.18-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 76.18"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Payment of Benefits]{.HTitleText3} {#h-983965 .Subheading}

- **[[76.19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.19
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  provincial benefits paid to a claimant in respect of a week in a
  benefit period are considered to be benefits paid in respect of a week
  under the Act if the claimant would have been entitled to the
  corresponding types of benefits under the Act, and any week in respect
  of which the claimant receives provincial benefits counts as a week
  for the purpose of calculating

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the overall maximum number of weeks for which
    benefits may be paid in a benefit period under paragraphs 12(3)(a)
    and (b) of the Act taken together; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the maximum number of weeks for which benefits may
    be paid under subsection 12(4) of the Act.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A benefit period is deemed to be established when a
  benefit period was established under a provincial law, and it is
  deemed to have begun the same week as the period established under the
  provincial law if the claimant would have been entitled to the
  corresponding types of benefits under the Act in respect of the same
  period.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a provincial plan provides for the optional
  payment of provincial benefits at an accelerated rate, such that the
  maximum amount of a particular type of provincial benefit payable
  under the plan may be paid to the claimant over fewer weeks, and the
  claimant is paid those benefits at the accelerated rate, the number of
  weeks of benefits that those weeks of provincial benefits represent
  under the Act shall be determined by multiplying the number of weeks
  of the particular type of provincial benefits paid to the claimant by
  the result obtained by dividing the maximum number of such weeks of
  benefits payable at the nonaccelerated rate by the maximum number of
  such weeks of benefits payable at the accelerated rate.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the determination of the number of weeks under
  subsection (2) results in a number that contains a fraction of a week,
  the number shall be rounded to the nearest multiple of one week or, if
  the amount is equidistant from two multiples of one week, to the
  higher multiple.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
- SOR/2010-301, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76.19-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 76.19"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[76.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.2 .sectionLabel}** If the maximum number
of weeks referred to in paragraph 12(3)(a) or (b) or (4)(a) or (b) of
the Act is less than the maximum number of weeks for which the
corresponding types of benefits may be paid under a provincial plan, the
excess number of weeks for which provincial benefits may be paid shall
not be taken into account for the purpose of determining the maximum
number of weeks referred to in that paragraph.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
:::

#### [Persons Making Claims Under Different Regimes]{.HTitleText3} {#h-983978 .Subheading}

- **[[76.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.21
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) applies in respect of
  two persons who are caring for the same child or children and who do
  not reside in the same province at the time the first one of them
  makes an application under section 22 or 23 of the Act or an
  application for provincial benefits.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), where one of the two
  persons referred to in subsection (1) has applied for and is entitled
  to receive benefits under section 23 of the Act (referred to in this
  section as "the claimant") and the other person has applied for and is
  entitled to receive provincial benefits (referred to in this section
  as "the provincial applicant"), unless they have entered into an
  agreement as to the number of weeks of such benefits they will each
  respectively apply for or there is a court order respecting the
  sharing of those weeks of benefits,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the number of weeks of benefits that the
    claimant would otherwise be entitled to receive under section 23 of
    the Act is an even number, the number of weeks of benefits payable
    to the claimant is half that number; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if that number is an odd number,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} where the claimant made the earlier application,
      one week of those benefits plus half of the remaining weeks of
      benefits is payable to the claimant, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where the provincial applicant made the earlier
      application, half the number of weeks of benefits remaining, after
      deducting one week, are payable to the claimant.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks of benefits that may be
  paid to the claimant under section 23 of the Act shall not be greater
  than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claimant who has, under subsection
    23(1.1) of the Act, elected to receive benefits for the maximum
    number of weeks referred to in subparagraph 12(3)(b)(i) of the Act,
    the maximum number of weeks referred to in subparagraph 12(4)(b)(i)
    of the Act when the weeks of benefits are divided, less the number
    of weeks of provincial benefits that are paid to the provincial
    applicant, taking into account any weeks of provincial benefits that
    are paid at the accelerated rate referred to in subsection 76.19(2),
    if applicable; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claimant who has, under subsection
    23(1.1) of the Act, elected to receive benefits for the maximum
    number of weeks referred to in subparagraph 12(3)(b)(ii) of the Act,
    the number of weeks, rounded down to the nearest whole number,
    determined by the following formula:

    ::: {#1119432 .Paragraph}
    (A -- B) × C/D

    where:

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the maximum number of weeks referred to in subparagraph
        12(4)(b)(i) of the Act when the weeks of benefits are divided;

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of weeks of provincial benefits that are paid to
        the provincial applicant, taking into account any weeks of
        provincial benefits that are paid at the accelerated rate
        referred to in subsection 76.19(2), if applicable;

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the maximum number of weeks referred to in subparagraph
        12(3)(b)(ii) of the Act; and

    [D]{.dfn}
    :   is the maximum number of weeks referred to in subparagraph
        12(3)(b)(i) of the Act.
    :::

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks for which benefits may be
  paid to a claimant under subsection (3) is the maximum number of weeks
  established under subparagraph 12(3)(b)(i) or (ii) of the Act, in
  accordance with the election made under subsection 23(1.1) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
- SOR/2017-226, s. 17
- [SOR/2019-58, s. 1](#1115593-1119429){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115593-1119429.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76.21-20171203.html "Link to previous version of section 76.21"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Waiving of the Waiting Period]{.HTitleText3} {#h-983996 .Subheading}

**[[76.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.22 .sectionLabel}** The reference in
paragraph 40(6)(b) to "allowances, payments or other moneys are payable
to the claimant by the claimant's employer or former employer as sick
leave pay" shall be read as a reference to "allowances, payments or
other moneys were payable to the claimant by the claimant's employer or
former employer as sick leave pay or provincial benefits were paid to
the claimant".

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
:::

#### [Coming Into Force of Provincial Plan --- Transition]{.HTitleText3} {#h-984000 .Subheading}

**[[76.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.23 .sectionLabel}** For greater
certainty,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a birth or a placement for the purpose of adoption
  takes place before the day on which this Part comes into force, the
  Act applies in respect of any claim for benefits made in respect of
  that birth or placement; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a claimant has, before the day on which this Part
  comes into force, been paid one week or more of benefits under section
  22 or 23 of the Act in respect of a birth or a placement for the
  purpose of adoption, the Act continues to apply in respect of any
  claim relating to that birth or placement.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
:::

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Premium And Adjustment Payments --- Provincial Plans]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984006 .Subheading}

#### [Premium for Insured Person Covered in a Province by a Provincial Plan but Working in Another Province or Working Outside Canada]{.HTitleText3} {#h-984007 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2006-350, s. 1

\]
:::

**[[76.24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.24 .sectionLabel}** Where an insured
person who is covered by a provincial plan is employed by an employer in
a province that has no provincial plan or is employed outside Canada,
the employer shall not take into account any premium reduction for the
purpose of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} deducting the amount required to be deducted as or on
  account of the employee's premiums by paragraph 82(1)(a) of the Act;
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} remitting the amount of the employer's premium
  required to be remitted in respect of that employee by paragraph
  82(1)(b) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
- SOR/2006-350, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76.24-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 76.24"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Premium for Insured Person Working in a Province that Has a Provincial Plan but Not Covered by that Plan]{.HTitleText3} {#h-984015 .Subheading}

**[[76.25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.25 .sectionLabel}** Where an insured
person is employed by an employer in a province that has a provincial
plan and the insured person is not covered by that plan, the employer
shall take into account any premium reduction for the purpose of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} deducting the amount required to be deducted as or on
  account of the employee's premiums by paragraph 82(1)(a) of the Act;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} remitting the amount of the employer's premium
  required to be remitted in respect of that employee by paragraph
  82(1)(b) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
:::

#### [Premium Adjustment for Employees Residing in a Province that Has a Provincial Plan but Working in a Province that Has No Provincial Plan or Working Outside Canada]{.HTitleText3} {#h-984021 .Subheading}

- **[[76.26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.26
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of National Revenue
  shall pay to a province that has a provincial plan a premium
  adjustment consisting of the aggregate of :

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount obtained by adding together, for all
    insured persons referred to in section 76.24 who reside in that
    province on December 31 of a year, the amounts calculated in respect
    of each such insured person that represent the lesser of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the total of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} all amounts that are deducted by employers in
        that year and that represent the premium reduction, as or on
        account of employee's premiums, for the insured person, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} all amounts representing an overpayment of
        employee's premiums under Part IV of the Act in respect of the
        insured person for that year, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all amounts that the insured person owes in that
      year under the provincial plan, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total of all amounts that represent the premium
    reduction and that are remitted by employers in that year as
    employer's premiums for every insured person referred to in section
    76.24 who resides in that province on December 31 of that year.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The payment referred to in subsection (1) may be made
  by the Commissioner of Revenue.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
- SOR/2006-350, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76.26-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 76.26"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Premium Adjustment --- Credits and Debits]{.HTitleText3} {#h-984033 .Subheading}

- **[[76.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any amount paid by a province to the
  Receiver General on account of a premium adjustment shall be
  considered to be an amount paid as or on account of premiums under
  paragraph 72(a) of the Act and shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} paid into the Consolidated Revenue Fund; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} credited to the Employment Insurance Operating
    Account and charged to the Consolidated Revenue Fund under paragraph
    73(a) of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any amount paid under section 76.26 to a province on
  account of a premium adjustment shall be paid out of the Consolidated
  Revenue Fund and charged to the Employment Insurance Operating Account
  as if it were an amount required to be paid out of that Fund and
  charged to that Account by subsection 77(1) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2203
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76.27-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 76.27"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Overpayment and Refund of Employee's Premiums]{.HTitleText3} {#h-984041 .Subheading}

- **[[76.28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.28
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a province that has a provincial
  plan has paid or is required to pay to the Receiver General a premium
  adjustment that corresponds to the amount of employee's premiums
  deducted in respect of an employee under that plan, that amount shall
  be taken into account as though the amount of the premium adjustment
  had been paid on account of the employee's premiums under the Act for
  the purpose of determining whether there has been an overpayment by
  the employee for the purposes of Part IV of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a premium adjustment referred to in subsection
  76.26(1) has been paid or is required to be paid to a province that
  has a provincial plan, the amount of that premium adjustment shall not
  be taken into account for the purposes of Part IV of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
- SOR/2006-350, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76.28-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 76.28"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Administrative Matters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984047 .Subheading}

#### [Social Insurance Number]{.HTitleText3} {#h-984048 .Subheading}

**[[76.29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.29 .sectionLabel}** A claimant's social
insurance number shall be used for the purpose of facilitating the
exchange, between the Government of Canada and a province, of
information obtained with respect to a claimant under provincial law or
the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
:::

#### [Disclosure of Information]{.HTitleText3} {#h-984052 .Subheading}

**[[76.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.3 .sectionLabel}** The Canada Revenue
Agency and the Minister of National Revenue are authorized to disclose
to a province that has a provincial plan information necessary for the
administration of this Part that has been obtained by the Agency or the
Minister under the Act or these Regulations and any information prepared
from that information.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-366, s. 1
- SOR/2006-350, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76.3-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 76.3"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART III.2]{.HLabel1}[Reduction of Premium for Self-Employed Persons Covered by a Plan Established Under a Provincial Law]{.HTitleText1} {#h-984056 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984057 .Subheading}

**[[76.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.31 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[plan established under a provincial law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[plan established under a provincial law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
    means a plan, established under a provincial law, that provides for
    the payment of provincial benefits and in respect of which the
    premium payable under section 152.21 of the Act is subject to a
    system for reducing that premium if the payment of allowances, money
    or other benefits payable under the provincial law would have the
    effect of reducing or eliminating benefits payable under section
    152.04 or 152.05 of the Act. ([régime établi en vertu d'une loi
    provinciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[provincial benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[provincial benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means allowances, money
    or other benefits, paid to a self-employed person under a plan
    established under a provincial law because of pregnancy or in
    respect of the care by that person of one or more of their new-born
    children or one or more children placed with them for the purpose of
    adoption. ([prestations provinciales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

### [Standards]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984063 .Subheading}

**[[76.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.32 .sectionLabel}** A plan established
under a provincial law shall meet the following requirements:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the plan must provide for the payment of provincial
  benefits;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the plan must, at a minimum, cover substantially the
  same persons as those who are self-employed persons under the Act;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the global amount of provincial benefits payable to a
  person under the plan must be substantially equivalent to or greater
  than the global amount of benefits payable to a self-employed person
  under section 152.04 or 152.05 of the Act;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the plan must provide that an applicant who has
  received provincial benefits in respect of at least one week under it
  shall continue to receive such benefits for their full period of
  entitlement, even if, after receiving provincial benefits in respect
  of one week, they become a resident of a different province; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the plan must provide for the sharing, by the
  province with the Government of Canada, of information obtained in the
  course of administering the plan that is necessary for the
  administration of a premium reduction system established by this Part,
  as well as information prepared from that information.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

### [Premium Reduction]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984072 .Subheading}

**[[76.33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.33 .sectionLabel}** The self-employment
premium payable under section 152.21 of the Act shall be reduced as
provided by section 76.34 if the payment of provincial benefits would
have the effect referred to in subsection 69(2) of the Act in respect of
benefits payable to those persons under section 152.04 or 152.05 of the
Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

**[[76.34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.34 .sectionLabel}** The premium
reduction to be applied to the self-employment premium shall be
calculated by applying the premium reduction rate determined in
accordance with section 76.06.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

**[[76.35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.35 .sectionLabel}** The premium
reduction rate of the self-employment premium determined under section
76.06 is to be published in Part 1 of the [Canada
Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) by the Commission as soon as
feasible after it is determined.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
- SOR/2015-128, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76.35-20110101.html "Link to previous version of section 76.35"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Disentitlement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984082 .Subheading}

- **[[76.36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.36
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a
  self-employed person is disentitled to be paid benefits under section
  152.04 or 152.05 of the Act if they are entitled to receive provincial
  benefits.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if, at the request of
  the self-employed person, it is determined by the Commission that the
  amount of provincial benefits that the self-employed person is
  entitled to receive is not substantially equivalent to or greater than
  the amount of benefits that they are entitled to receive under section
  152.04 or 152.05 of the Act.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every self-employed person who has received, or has
  applied for and is entitled to receive, provincial benefits in respect
  of any week is disentitled to be paid benefits in respect of that same
  week under Part VII.1 of the Act, other than benefits under section
  152.04 or 152.05 of the Act.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, subsections (1) to (3) apply
  in respect of a self-employed person who has applied for and is
  entitled to receive provincial benefits even if the self-employed
  person, after making that application, ceases to reside in the
  province providing those benefits.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, if two persons are caring for
  the same child or children and one of them is a self-employed person
  referred to in subsection (4),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsections (1) to (3) apply in respect of the
    other person if that other person is a self-employed person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsections 76.09(1) to (3) apply in respect of the
    other person if that other person is an insured person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

### [Reduction of Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984093 .Subheading}

**[[76.37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.37 .sectionLabel}** The benefits
payable under section 152.04 or 152.05 of the Act to a self-employed
person in respect of any week in respect of which the claimant has
received or is entitled to receive provincial benefits shall be reduced
as provided by section 76.38.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

**[[76.38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.38 .sectionLabel}** An amount equal to
the amount of the provincial benefits that a self-employed person has
received or is entitled to receive in respect of any week shall be
deducted from the amount of benefits payable under section 152.04 or
152.05 of the Act to the self-employed person in respect of that same
week, in addition to any deductions referred to in subsections 152.04(4)
and 152.05(11) and section 152.18 of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

### [No Double Counting of Weeks]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984100 .Subheading}

**[[76.39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.39 .sectionLabel}** If provincial
benefits paid in respect of a week have been taken into account for the
purpose of section 76.4, no benefits paid under section 152.04 or 152.05
of the Act in respect of a week shall be taken into account for the same
purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

### [Payment of Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984104 .Subheading}

- **[[76.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), the
  provincial benefits paid to a self-employed person in respect of a
  week in a benefit period are considered to be benefits paid in respect
  of a week under the Act if the self-employed person would have been
  entitled to the corresponding types of benefits under the Act, and any
  week in respect of which the self-employed person receives provincial
  benefits counts as a week for the purpose of calculating

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the overall maximum number of weeks for which
    benefits may be paid in a benefit period under paragraphs
    152.14(1)(a) and (b) of the Act taken together; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the maximum number of weeks for which benefits may
    be paid under subsection 152.14(2) of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A benefit period is deemed to be established when a
  benefit period was established under a provincial law, and it is
  deemed to have begun the same week as the period established under the
  provincial law if the self-employed person would have been entitled to
  the corresponding types of benefits under the Act in respect of the
  same period.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the provincial benefits may be paid at an
  accelerated rate, such that the maximum amount of a particular type of
  provincial benefit may be paid to a self-employed person over fewer
  weeks, and the self-employed person is paid those benefits at the
  accelerated rate, the number of weeks of benefits that those weeks of
  provincial benefits represent under the Act shall be determined by
  multiplying the number of weeks of the particular type of provincial
  benefits paid to the self-employed person by the result obtained by
  dividing the maximum number of such weeks of benefits payable at the
  non-accelerated rate by the maximum number of such weeks of benefits
  payable at the accelerated rate.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the determination of the number of weeks under
  subsection (3) results in a number that contains a fraction of a week,
  the number shall be rounded to the nearest multiple of one week or, if
  the amount is equidistant from two multiples of one week, to the
  higher multiple.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

**[[76.41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.41 .sectionLabel}** If the maximum
number of weeks referred to in paragraph 152.14(1)(a) or (b) or (2)(a)
or (b) of the Act is less than the maximum number of weeks for which the
corresponding types of provincial benefits may be paid, the excess
number of weeks for which provincial benefits may be paid shall not be
taken into account for the purpose of determining the maximum number of
weeks referred to in that paragraph.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

### [Persons Making Claims Under Different Regimes]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984117 .Subheading}

- **[[76.42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.42
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) applies in respect of
  two persons who are caring for the same child or children and who do
  not reside in the same province at the time the first one of them
  makes an application under section 152.04 or 152.05 of the Act or an
  application for provincial benefits.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), if one of the two persons
  referred to in subsection (1) has applied for and is entitled to
  receive benefits under section 152.05 of the Act (referred to in this
  section as "the self-employed person") and the other person has
  applied for and is entitled to receive provincial benefits (referred
  to in this section as "the provincial applicant"), unless they have
  entered into an agreement as to the number of weeks of such benefits
  they will each respectively apply for or there is a court order
  respecting the sharing of those weeks of benefits,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the number of weeks of benefits that the
    self-employed person would otherwise be entitled to receive under
    section 152.05 of the Act is an even number, the number of weeks of
    benefits payable to the self-employed person is half that number;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if that number is an odd number,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the self-employed person made the earlier
      application, one week of those benefits plus half of the remaining
      weeks of benefits is payable to the self-employed person, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the provincial applicant made the earlier
      application, half the number of weeks of benefits remaining, after
      deducting one week, are payable to the self-employed person.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks of benefits that may be
  paid to the self-employed person under section 152.05 of the Act shall
  not be greater than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a self-employed person who has,
    under subsection 152.05(1.1) of the Act, elected to receive benefits
    for the maximum number of weeks referred to in subparagraph
    152.14(1)(b)(i) of the Act, the maximum number of weeks referred to
    in subparagraph 152.14(2)(b)(i) of the Act when the weeks of
    benefits are divided, less the number of weeks of provincial
    benefits that are paid to the provincial applicant, taking into
    account any weeks of provincial benefits that are paid at the
    accelerated rate referred to in subsection 76.4(3), if applicable;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a self-employed person who has,
    under subsection 152.05(1.1) of the Act, elected to receive benefits
    for the maximum number of weeks referred to in subparagraph
    152.14(1)(b)(ii) of the Act, the number of weeks, rounded down to
    the nearest whole number, determined by the following formula:

    ::: {#1119442 .Paragraph}
    (A -- B) × C/D

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the maximum number of weeks referred to in subparagraph
        152.14(2)(b)(i) of the Act when the weeks of benefits are
        divided;

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of weeks of provincial benefits that are paid to
        the provincial applicant, taking into account any weeks of
        provincial benefits that are paid at the accelerated rate
        referred to in subsection 76.4(3), if applicable;

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the maximum number of weeks referred to in subparagraph
        152.14(1)(b)(ii) of the Act; and

    [D]{.dfn}
    :   is the maximum number of weeks referred to in subparagraph
        152.14(1)(b)(i) of the Act.
    :::

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks for which benefits may be
  paid to a claimant under subsection (3) is the maximum number of weeks
  established under subparagraph 152.14(1)(b)(i) or (ii) of the Act, in
  accordance with the election made under subsection 152.05(1.1) of the
  Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
- SOR/2017-226, s. 18
- [SOR/2019-58, s. 2](#1115598-1119439){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115598-1119439.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76.42-20171203.html "Link to previous version of section 76.42"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Waiving of the Waiting Period]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984135 .Subheading}

**[[76.421]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.421 .sectionLabel}** The reference in
paragraph 40(6)(b) to "allowances, payments or other moneys are payable
to the claimant by the claimant's employer or former employer as sick
leave pay" shall be read as a reference to "allowances, payments or
other moneys were payable to the claimant by the claimant's employer or
former employer as sick leave pay or provincial benefits were paid to
the claimant".

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-26, s. 2
:::

### [Interprovincial Mobility]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984139 .Subheading}

**[[76.43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.43 .sectionLabel}** A self-employed
person who has ceased to reside in a province that has a plan
established under a provincial law and who entered into an agreement
under section 152.02 of the Act within 12 months before the day on which
they ceased to reside in the province or within three months after that
day is deemed, in order to qualify for the benefits payable under
sections 152.04 and 152.05 of the Act, to have satisfied the condition
set out in paragraph 152.07(1)(a) of the Act if they were covered by
that plan during the 12 months before the day on which they ceased to
reside in the province.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

### [Administration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984143 .Subheading}

**[[76.44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.44 .sectionLabel}** A self-employed
person's social insurance number shall be used for the purpose of
facilitating the exchange, between the Government of Canada and a
province, of information obtained with respect to a self-employed person
under provincial law or the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

**[[76.45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.45 .sectionLabel}** The Canada Revenue
Agency and the Minister of National Revenue are authorized to disclose
to a province that has a plan established under a provincial law
information necessary for the administration of this Part that has been
obtained by that Agency or that Minister under the Act or these
Regulations and any information prepared from that information.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-301, s. 9
:::

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Pilot Projects]{.HTitleText1} {#h-984150 .Part}

### [Pilot Project for Exemption from the Requirement to Make Periodic Claims for Benefits for Weeks of Unemployment in the Benefit Period]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984151 .Subheading}

- **[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission shall establish Pilot Project No. 1 for the purpose of
  assessing the cost, the validity of payment, the operational impact
  and the effect on customer service to claimants of exempting them from
  the requirement in sections 49 and 50 of the Act to make periodic
  claims for benefits.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The definitions in this subsection apply in this
  section.

  [[condition of entitlement to benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[condition of entitlement to benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      any requirement, circumstance or condition referred to in
      subsection 49(1) of the Act. ([condition d\'admissibilité au
      bénéfice des prestations]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[period of eligibility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[period of eligibility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the benefits referred to in
        subparagraph (3) (d) (i)

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate of the waiting period referred
          to in section 13 of the Act and the period described in
          subsection 22(2) of the Act and any extension of the period
          described in subsection 22(2) of the Act, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate of the period referred to in
          subsection 23(2) of the Act and the waiting period referred to
          in section 13 of the Act, unless that waiting period has
          already been taken into account for the purposes of
          subparagraph (i); and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the benefits referred to in
        subparagraph (3)(d)(ii), the period during which the claimant
        attends a course or program, referred to in paragraph 25(1)(a)
        of the Act, that is an apprenticeship course or an
        apprenticeship program. ([période
        d\'admissibilité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claimant is eligible to participate in Pilot
  Project No. 1 where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant resides in Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant makes an initial claim for benefits or
    a claim in respect of which subsection 26(2) applies;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claimant\'s period of eligibility begins on or
    after June 30, 1996 and ends not later than June 26, 1999; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the claimant makes a claim for benefits in respect
    of every week of unemployment in the claimant\'s period of
    eligibility

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for either of the reasons referred to in
      paragraphs 12(3)(a) and (b) of the Act, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} under section 25 of the Act, to attend an
      apprenticeship course or an apprenticeship program.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who is eligible to participate in Pilot
  Project No. 1 and who wishes to participate in the project shall
  complete and sign a form, supplied by the Commission, agreeing that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to the best of the claimant\'s knowledge at the
    time of signing the form, there are no conditions of entitlement to
    benefits that will not be fully met for each week in the period of
    eligibility following the waiting period, except in respect of
    earnings that may be deducted pursuant to section 19, 22 or 23 of
    the Act during that period; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} should the claimant cease to meet a condition of
    entitlement to benefits at any time during the period of eligibility
    where failure to meet that condition has the effect of reducing or
    eliminating any benefits for any week in the period of eligibility,
    the claimant will notify the Commission of the condition as soon as
    possible.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of sections 49 and 50 of the Act, a
  claimant who participates in Pilot Project No. 1 is, for the purpose
  of this section, deemed to have made a claim for benefits for every
  week of unemployment in the claimant\'s period of eligibility.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Where the Commission becomes aware that the claimant
  does not meet a condition of entitlement to benefits during the
  claimant\'s period of eligibility, the Commission may terminate the
  claimant\'s participation in Pilot Project No. 1 as of the date on
  which the Commission becomes aware of the claimant\'s failure to meet
  the condition.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (3)(a) ceases to be in force on April 1,
  1997.

### [Pilot Project Extending the Benefit Period of Certain Claimants]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984174 .Subheading}

- **[[77.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Commission establishes Pilot
  Project No. 5 for the purpose of assessing the effect of allowing
  claimants who are entitled to benefits for one or more weeks for which
  they receive or are entitled to receive an indemnity payment described
  in paragraph 35(2)(f) to choose not to be paid benefits for those
  weeks.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 5 includes all claimants

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whose benefit period begins or ends during the
    Pilot Project;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} who receive or are entitled to receive an indemnity
    payment described in paragraph 35(2)(f) during their benefit period;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} who are entitled to benefits for one or more weeks
    for which they receive or are entitled to receive an indemnity
    payment described in paragraph 35(2)(f).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who chooses not to be paid benefits to
  which they are entitled for one or more weeks for which they receive
  or are entitled to receive an indemnity payment described in paragraph
  35(2)(f) is, for the purposes of paragraph 10(10)(d) of the Act,
  deemed to have proved that they are not entitled to benefits for those
  weeks.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The following applies to a claimant who, after being
  paid benefits for one or more weeks for which they received or were
  entitled to receive an indemnity payment described in paragraph
  35(2)(f), chooses not to be paid benefits:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the Commission informed the claimant about Pilot
    Project No. 5 before the claimant was paid benefits, subsection (3)
    does not apply in respect of the week or weeks for which a benefit
    was paid; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Commission did not inform the claimant about
    the Pilot Project before the claimant was paid benefits,
    subsection (3) applies in respect of the week or weeks for which a
    benefit was paid and the claimant must repay the benefits paid.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A claimant may rescind a choice made by them under
  subsection (3). A claimant who rescinds shall be paid benefits to
  which they are entitled.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 5 does not apply to a week
  preceding the week in which this section comes into force or a week
  following the week in which this section ceases to have effect.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-364, s. 3
:::

### [Pilot Project Relating to Increased Weeks of Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984189 .Subheading}

- **[[77.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Commission establishes Pilot
  Project No. 6 for the purpose of assessing the costs and impact of
  increasing weeks of benefits in selected economic regions.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 6 includes each claimant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whose benefit period is established in the period
    beginning on June 6, 2004 and ending on June 4, 2006; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} who, at the time the benefit period is established,
    is ordinarily resident in a region described in Schedule I that is
    set out in Schedule II.1.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 12(2) of the Act, the maximum
  number of weeks for which benefits may be paid in a benefit period
  that is established for a claimant who is included in Pilot Project
  No. 6 shall be determined in accordance with the table set out in
  Schedule II.2 by reference to the regional rate of unemployment that
  applies to the claimant and the number of hours of insurable
  employment of the claimant in their qualifying period.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-146, s. 1

### [Pilot Project for Calculating Benefit Rate Based On Claimant's 14 Highest Weeks of Insurable Earnings (2)]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984202 .Subheading}

- **[[77.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 11 is established
  for the purpose of testing whether paying benefits based on
  calculating the rate of weekly benefits using the insurable earnings
  from a claimant's 14 highest weeks of insurable earnings in the
  qualifying period would encourage claimants to accept all available
  work.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 11 applies in respect of every
  claimant whose benefit period is established in the period beginning
  on October 26, 2008 and ending on June 25, 2011 and who is ordinarily
  resident in a region described in Schedule I that is set out in
  Schedule II.8, other than a claimant in respect of whom the
  [Employment Insurance (Fishing)
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-445) apply.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of Pilot Project No. 11,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsections 14(2), (4) and (4.1) of the Act do not
    apply;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the reference in subsection 14(3) of the Act to
    "the rate calculation period" shall be read as a reference to "the
    qualifying period";

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the references in section 24.1 of these Regulations
    to "the rate calculation period" shall be read as references to "the
    qualifying period";

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} section 24.2 of these Regulations does not apply;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the insurable earnings in a claimant's qualifying
    period shall be the aggregate of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the insurable earnings from the 14 highest weeks
      of insurable earnings in that period, not including any insurable
      earnings paid or payable to the claimant in the qualifying period
      under section 24.1, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any insurable earnings paid or payable to the
      claimant in the qualifying period under section 24.1; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a claimant's weekly insurable earnings shall be
    determined by dividing the insurable earnings in the claimant's
    qualifying period, determined in accordance with paragraph (e), by
    14.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant's insurable earnings have been reported
  on the record of employment by pay period, the Commission shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} allocate the amount of insurable earnings
    proportionately over the pay period; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the claimant or the employer provides evidence
    of the amount of insurable earnings actually earned by the claimant
    in any week within the pay period, allocate the amount of insurable
    earnings proportionately over the other weeks in that pay period.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2008-257, s. 2
- SOR/2010-214, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-77.7-20081026.html "Link to previous version of section 77.7"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Pilot Project Increasing Allowable Earnings from Employment While Claimant is Receiving Benefits (2)]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984220 .Subheading}

- **[[77.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 12 is established
  for the purpose of testing whether increasing the amount of a
  claimant's allowable earnings from employment while the claimant is
  receiving benefits would encourage more claimants to accept employment
  while receiving benefits.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 12 applies in respect of every
  claimant whose benefit period is established or ends in the period
  beginning on December 7, 2008 and ending on August 6, 2011 and who is
  ordinarily resident in a region described in Schedule I.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of Pilot Project No. 12, subsection
  19(2) of the Act is adapted such that the maximum allowable earnings
  shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$75, if the claimant's rate of weekly benefits is
    less than \$188; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 40% of the claimant's rate of weekly benefits, if
    that rate is \$188 or more.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, this section ceases to have
  effect on August 6, 2011.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2008-257, s. 2
- SOR/2010-214, s. 3
:::

### [Pilot Project Relating to Extended Employment Insurance and Training Incentive]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984231 .Subheading}

- **[[77.91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.91
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 14 is established
  for the purposes of assessing whether the extension of the number of
  weeks of benefits paid to a qualified claimant who undertakes
  long-term training encourages the claimant to pursue such training and
  improves their re-employability.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 14 applies in respect of every
  claimant whose benefit period is established in the period beginning
  on January 25, 2009 and ending on May 29, 2010.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 14 applies in respect of every
  claimant who meets the following criteria:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant contributed at least 30% of the
    maximum annual employee's premium in at least seven of the 10 years
    before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant was paid less than 36 weeks of regular
    benefits in the 260 weeks before the beginning of the claimant's
    benefit period;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} within 20 weeks after the beginning of the
    claimant's benefit period or before August 23, 2009, if the benefit
    period began before May 31, 2009, the claimant has an active return
    to work action plan; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the claimant is referred by the Commission, or an
    authority that the Commission designates, under paragraph 25(1)(a)
    of the Act, to a course or program of instruction or training

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is full-time,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that has a duration of at least 20 weeks, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} that begins during one of the 52 weeks
      following the beginning of the claimant's benefit period, but not
      before May 31, 2009.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections 10(2) and (8) of the Act, the
  benefit period of a claimant who is included in Pilot Project No. 14
  is extended by the duration, in weeks, of the course or program
  referred to in paragraph (3)(d) --- including any periods of
  interruption of the course or program --- and the period granted for
  job-search purposes, up to a maximum of 104 weeks.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks for which benefits may be
  paid to a claimant who is included in Pilot Project No. 14 is that
  which is determined by any of subsections 12(2), (3) or (6) of the Act
  added to, if applicable, the number of weeks that fall within the
  benefit period established under subsection (4)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} during which the claimant is attending the course
    or program referred to in paragraph (3)(d) or is searching for a job
    during the period granted for that purpose; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for which no benefits are payable under subsection
    12(1) of the Act.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the application of subsections (4) and (5), the
  period granted for job-search purposes is equal to one week of
  benefits for every five weeks of training completed, up to a maximum
  of 12 consecutive weeks. This period begins on the Sunday after the
  last day on which the claimant attends the course or program.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} If any of the conditions under which the Commission
  may terminate the claimant's referral under paragraph 27(1.1)(b) of
  the Act exists, subsection (5) ceases to apply on the Sunday after the
  last day on which the claimant attends the course or program.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2009-130, s. 2
- SOR/2009-233, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-77.91-20090531.html "Link to previous version of section 77.91"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Pilot Project Relating to Extended Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984251 .Subheading}

- **[[77.92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.92
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 15 is established
  for the purpose of assessing the costs and impact of extending the
  number of weeks of benefits in selected economic regions.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 15 applies in respect of every
  claimant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} who, at the time that their benefit period is
    established, is ordinarily resident in a region described in
    Schedule I that is set out in Schedule II.6; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whose benefit period is established in the period
    beginning on September 12, 2010 and ending

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the regional rate of unemployment, as
      determined in accordance with subsection 17(1), is less than 8.0%
      for 12 consecutive periods, beginning after October 9, 2010, in
      the region in respect of which the benefit period was established,
      on the second Saturday after the first day of the 12th period, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, on September 15, 2012.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the condition referred to in subparagraph
  (2)(b)(i) is satisfied, the Commission must, as soon as possible,
  publish a notice in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)
  that specifies the day on which the establishment of benefit periods
  ends, in the region in question, under Pilot Project No. 15. The
  Commission must also publish the notice on its website.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 12(2) of the Act, the maximum
  number of weeks for which benefits may be paid in a benefit period
  that is established for a claimant who is included in Pilot Project
  No. 15 shall be determined in accordance with the table set out in
  Schedule II.7 by reference to the regional rate of unemployment that
  applies to the claimant and the number of hours of insurable
  employment of the claimant in their qualifying period.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-214, s. 4
:::

### [Pilot Project for Calculating Benefit Rate Based on Claimant's 14 Highest Weeks of Insurable Earnings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984263 .Subheading}

- **[[77.93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.93
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 16 is established
  for the purpose of testing whether paying benefits based on a rate of
  weekly benefits calculated using the insurable earnings from a
  claimant's 14 highest weeks of insurable earnings in the qualifying
  period would encourage claimants to accept all available work.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 16 applies in respect of every
  claimant whose benefit period is established in the period beginning
  on June 26, 2011 and ending on April 6, 2013 and who is ordinarily
  resident in a region described in Schedule I that is set out in
  Schedule II.91, other than a claimant in respect of whom Part VII.1 of
  the Act applies or in respect of whom the [Employment Insurance
  (Fishing) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-445) apply.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of Pilot Project No. 16,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsections 14(2), (4) and (4.1) of the Act do not
    apply;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the reference in subsection 14(3) of the Act to
    "the rate calculation period" shall be read as a reference to "the
    qualifying period";

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the references in section 24.1 to "the rate
    calculation period" shall be read as references to "the qualifying
    period";

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} section 24.2 does not apply;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the insurable earnings in a claimant's qualifying
    period shall be the aggregate of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the insurable earnings from the 14 highest weeks
      of insurable earnings in that period, not including any insurable
      earnings paid or payable to the claimant in the qualifying period
      under section 24.1, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any insurable earnings paid or payable to the
      claimant in the qualifying period under section 24.1; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a claimant's weekly insurable earnings shall be
    determined by dividing the insurable earnings in the claimant's
    qualifying period, determined in accordance with paragraph (e), by
    14.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant's insurable earnings have been reported
  on the record of employment by pay period, the Commission shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} allocate the amount of insurable earnings
    proportionately over the pay period; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the claimant or the employer provides evidence
    of the amount of insurable earnings actually earned by the claimant
    in any week within the pay period, allocate the amount of insurable
    earnings proportionately over the other weeks in that pay period.

### [Pilot Project Increasing Allowable Earnings from Employment While Claimant is Receiving Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984281 .Subheading}

- **[[77.94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.94
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 17 is established
  for the purpose of testing whether increasing the amount of a
  claimant's allowable earnings from employment while the claimant is

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of Pilot Project No. 17, subsection

### [Pilot Project to Encourage Claimant to Work More While Receiving Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984291 .Subheading}

- **[[77.95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.95
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 18 is established
  for the purpose of testing whether deducting from benefits payable to
  any claimant who has earnings during a week of unemployment 50% of
  those earnings, until the earnings exceed 90% of their weekly
  insurable earnings, would encourage claimants to work more while
  receiving benefits.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 18 applies in respect of every
  claimant who makes a claim for benefits for any week in the period
  beginning on August 5, 2012 and ending on August 1, 2015 and who is
  ordinarily resident in a region described in Schedule I.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of Pilot Project No. 18, section 19
  of the Act is adapted by adding the following after subsection (2):

  > ::: ReadAsText
  > - [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The amount to be deducted under subsection (2),
  >   except for the purpose of section 13, is equal to the total of
  >
  >   - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 50% of the earnings that are less than or equal
  >     to 90% of the claimant's weekly insurable earnings used to
  >     establish their rate of weekly benefits, and
  >
  >   - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 100% of any earnings that are greater than 90%
  >     of the claimant's weekly insurable earnings used to establish
  >     their rate of weekly benefits.
  > :::

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of Pilot Project No. 18, section
  152.18 of the Act is adapted by adding the following after subsection
  (2):

  > ::: ReadAsText
  > - [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The amount to be deducted under subsection (2),
  >   except for the purpose of section 152.15, is equal to the total of
  >
  >   - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 50% of the earnings that are less than or equal
  >     to 90% of the sum of the amounts referred to in paragraphs
  >     152.16(1)(a) and (b) divided by 52, and
  >
  >   - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 100% of any earnings that are greater than 90%
  >     of the sum of the amounts referred to in paragraphs 152.16(1)(a)
  >     and (b) divided by 52.
  > :::

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} This section ceases to have effect on August 1, 2015.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-128, s. 2
:::

- **[[77.96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.96
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The purpose of Pilot Project No. 18
  is also to test which method, the one described in subsection 77.94(3)
  or the one described in subsection 77.95(3), is more effective in
  encouraging claimants to work more while receiving benefits.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who had earnings that were subject to
  subsection 77.94(3) during the period beginning on August 7, 2011 and
  ending on August 4, 2012 may elect to have subsection 77.94(3),
  instead of subsection 77.95(3), apply to earnings received during all
  weeks of unemployment included in a benefit period, or the portion of
  a benefit period, that falls within the period beginning on August 5,
  2012 and ending on August 1, 2015. The election is irrevocable.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claimant must inform the Commission of their
  election, if any, in respect of earnings received during all weeks of
  unemployment included in a given benefit period by the applicable
  deadline as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a notification of payment or non-payment of
    benefits in respect of one or more weeks of unemployment included in
    that benefit period is issued to the claimant during the period
    beginning on August 5, 2012 and ending on January 5, 2013, February
    5, 2013 or 30 days after the day on which the last notification in
    respect of one or more weeks of unemployment included in that
    benefit period is issued, whichever is later; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a notification of payment or non-payment of
    benefits in respect of one or more weeks of unemployment included in
    that benefit period is issued to the claimant after January 5, 2013,
    30 days after the day on which the last notification in respect of
    one or more weeks of unemployment included in that benefit period is
    issued or August 1, 2015, whichever is earlier.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant informs the Commission of their
  election after the applicable deadline, the election is considered to
  have been made by that deadline if the claimant shows that there was
  good cause for the delay throughout the period beginning on the day on
  which the deadline expired and ending on the day on which the claimant
  informs the Commission of their election.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (6), if no election is made by
  the applicable deadline, subsection 77.95(3) applies in respect of
  earnings received during all weeks of unemployment included in the
  benefit period in question and earnings received during all weeks of
  unemployment included in any subsequent benefit period. If a benefit
  period ends after August 1, 2015, subsection 77.95(3) applies only in
  respect of earnings received during all weeks of unemployment included
  in the portion of that benefit period that falls within the period
  beginning on August 5, 2012 and ending on August 1, 2015.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant has no earnings during the weeks of
  unemployment included in the benefit period in question, the failure
  to make an election by the applicable deadline does not result in the
  application of subsection 77.95(3) in respect of earnings received
  during all weeks of unemployment included in any subsequent benefit
  period.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (5), if, during the period
  beginning on August 5, 2012 and ending on October 6, 2012, a
  notification of payment or non-payment of benefits is issued to a
  claimant in respect of one or more weeks of unemployment included in a
  benefit period and, after October 6, 2012, no additional notifications
  are issued to the claimant in respect of one or more weeks of
  unemployment included in that same benefit period, the claimant may,
  if no election is made in respect of earnings received during all
  weeks of unemployment included in that benefit period, make an
  election in respect of earnings received during all weeks of
  unemployment included in the subsequent benefit period.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} A decision of the Commission in respect of any matter
  related to an election, including the failure to make an election, is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} until March 31, 2013, not subject to appeal under
    section 114 of the Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on or after April 1, 2013, not subject to
    reconsideration under section 112 of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-297, s. 1
:::

### [Pilot Project to Encourage Claimant to Work More While Receiving Benefits (2)]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984323 .Subheading}
