- **[[77.98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.98
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The purpose of Pilot Project No. 19
  is also to test which method, the one described in subsection 77.94(3)
  or the one described in subsection 77.97(3), is more effective in
  encouraging claimants to work more while receiving benefits.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claimant may elect to have subsection 77.94(3),
  instead of subsection 77.97(3), apply to earnings received during all
  weeks of unemployment included in a benefit period, or the portion of
  a benefit period, that falls within the period beginning on August 2,
  2015 and ending on August 6, 2016 if the claimant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} had earnings that were subject to subsection
    77.94(3) during the period beginning on August 7, 2011 and ending on
    August 4, 2012; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} made every election that they were entitled to make
    under section 77.96, other than an election in respect of a benefit
    period that began before August 2, 2015 if the last notification of
    payment or non-payment of benefits in respect of one or more weeks
    of unemployment included in that benefit period is issued to the
    claimant on or after August 2, 2015.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any election made under section 77.96 in respect of a
  benefit period is deemed to apply to earnings received during all
  weeks of unemployment included in the portion of that benefit period
  that falls within the period beginning on August 2, 2015 and ending on
  August 6, 2016 if the last notification of payment or non-payment of
  benefits in respect of one or more weeks of unemployment included in
  that benefit period is issued to the claimant on or after August 2,
  2015.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any election made under subsection (2) in respect of
  a benefit period that began before August 2, 2015 also applies to
  earnings received during all weeks of unemployment included in the
  portion of that benefit period that falls before August 2, 2015.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A claimant must inform the Commission of their
  election, if any, in respect of earnings received during all weeks of
  unemployment included in a given benefit period not later than 30 days
  after the day on which the last notification of payment or non-payment
  of benefits in respect of one or more weeks of unemployment included
  in that benefit period is issued to the claimant or August 6, 2016,
  whichever is earlier. The election is irrevocable.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant informs the Commission of their
  election after the day on which the deadline expires, the election is
  considered to have been made by that deadline if the claimant shows
  that there was good cause for the delay throughout the period
  beginning on the day on which the deadline expired and ending on the
  day on which the claimant informs the Commission of their election.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (8), if no election is made by
  the applicable deadline, subsection 77.97(3) applies in respect of
  earnings received during all weeks of unemployment included in the
  benefit period in question and earnings received during all weeks of
  unemployment included in any subsequent benefit period. If a benefit
  period ends after August 6, 2016, subsection 77.97(3) applies only in
  respect of earnings received during all weeks of unemployment included
  in the portion of that benefit period that falls within the period
  beginning on August 2, 2015 and ending on August 6, 2016.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant has no earnings during the weeks of
  unemployment included in the benefit period in question, the failure
  to make an election by the applicable deadline does not result in the
  application of subsection 77.97(3) in respect of earnings received
  during all weeks of unemployment included in any subsequent benefit
  period.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} A decision of the Commission in respect of any matter
  related to an election, including the failure to make an election, is
  not subject to reconsideration under section 112 of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-151, s. 1
:::

### [Pilot Project Relating to Earned Income While Receiving Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984354 .Subheading}

- **[[77.99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.99
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 20 is established
  for the purpose of testing whether deducting from benefits payable to
  any claimant who has earnings during a week of unemployment 50% of
  those earnings, until the earnings are greater than 90% of their
  weekly insurable earnings, would encourage claimants to work more
  while receiving benefits.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 20 applies in respect of every
  claimant who makes a claim for benefits for any week in the period
  beginning on August 7, 2016 and ending on August 11, 2018 and who is
  ordinarily resident in a region described in Schedule I.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of Pilot Project No. 20, section 19
  of the Act is adapted by adding the following after subsection (2):

  > ::: ReadAsText
  > - [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The amount to be deducted under subsection (2),
  >   except for the purpose of section 13, is equal to the total of
  >
  >   - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 50% of the earnings that are less than or equal
  >     to 90% of the claimant's weekly insurable earnings used to
  >     establish their rate of weekly benefits, and
  >
  >   - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 100% of any earnings that are greater than 90%
  >     of the claimant's weekly insurable earnings used to establish
  >     their rate of weekly benefits.
  > :::

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of Pilot Project No. 20, section
  152.18 of the Act is adapted by adding the following after subsection
  (2):

  > ::: ReadAsText
  > - [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The amount to be deducted under subsection (2),
  >   except for the purpose of section 152.15, is equal to the total of
  >
  >   - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 50% of the earnings that are less than or equal
  >     to 90% of the sum of the amounts referred to in paragraphs
  >     152.16(1)(a) and (b) divided by 52, and
  >
  >   - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 100% of any earnings that are greater than 90%
  >     of the sum of the amounts referred to in paragraphs 152.16(1)(a)
  >     and (b) divided by 52.
  > :::

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} This section ceases to have effect on August 11,
  2018.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-162, s. 3
:::

- **[[77.991]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.991
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The purpose of Pilot Project No. 20
  is also to test which method, the one described in subsection 77.94(3)
  or the one described in subsection 77.99(3), is more effective in
  encouraging claimants to work more while receiving benefits.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claimant may elect to have subsection 77.94(3),
  instead of subsection 77.99(3), apply to earnings received during all
  weeks of unemployment included in a benefit period, or the portion of
  a benefit period, that falls within the period beginning on August 7,
  2016 and ending on August 11, 2018.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any election made under section 77.98 in respect of a
  benefit period is deemed to apply to earnings received during all
  weeks of unemployment included in the portion of that benefit period
  that falls within the period beginning on August 7, 2016 and ending on
  August 11, 2018 if the last notification of payment or non-payment of
  benefits in respect of one or more weeks of unemployment included in
  that benefit period is issued to the claimant on or after August 7,
  2016.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who was eligible to make an election under
  section 77.98 in respect of a benefit period that began before August
  7, 2016 and has not ended on or before that day may make an election
  under subsection (2) in respect of that benefit period if the last
  notification of payment or non-payment of benefits in respect of that
  benefit period is issued to the claimant on or after August 7, 2016.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A claimant must inform the Commission of their
  election, if any, in respect of earnings received during all weeks of
  unemployment included in a given benefit period not later than the
  30th day after the day on which the last notification of payment or
  non-payment of benefits in respect of one or more weeks of
  unemployment included in that benefit period is issued to the claimant
  or August 11, 2018, whichever is earlier. The election is irrevocable.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant informs the Commission of their
  election after the day on which the deadline expires, the election is
  considered to have been made by that deadline if the claimant shows
  that there was good cause for the delay throughout the period
  beginning on the day on which the deadline expired and ending on the
  day on which the claimant informs the Commission of their election.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} A decision of the Commission in respect of any matter
  related to an election, including the failure to make an election, is
  not subject to reconsideration under section 112 of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-162, s. 3
:::

### [Pilot Project Relating to Increased Weeks of Benefits for Seasonal Workers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984381 .Subheading}

- **[[77.992]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.992
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 21 is established
  for the purpose of testing the effectiveness of a mechanism that
  targets persons who meet the criteria set out in subsection (2) and
  testing the outcomes of increasing the number of weeks of benefits
  paid to the targeted population.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 21 applies to every claimant who
  meets the following criteria:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date on which a benefit period is established
    falls within the period beginning on August 5, 2018 and ending on
    September 25, 2021;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the time that the benefit period is established,
    the claimant is ordinarily resident in a region described in
    Schedule I that is set out in Schedule II.92;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the 260 weeks before the beginning of the
    benefit period referred to in paragraph (a), at least three benefit
    periods had been established during which regular benefits were paid
    or payable; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} at least two of the benefit periods referred to in
    paragraph (c) had begun around the same time of year as the benefit
    period referred to in paragraph (a) began.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (2)(c), a claimant's
  benefit period established before the beginning of the 260-week period
  is considered to have been established within the 260-week period if
  the claimant received a notification of payment or non-payment with
  respect to any week that falls within that 260-week period.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (2)(d), a benefit
  period in a previous year is considered to have begun around the same
  time of year if it began during the period that begins eight weeks
  before and ends eight weeks after the week that is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 52 weeks before the first week of the benefit
    period referred to in paragraph (2)(a);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 104 weeks before the first week of the benefit
    period referred to in paragraph (2)(a);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 156 weeks before the first week of the benefit
    period referred to in paragraph (2)(a);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} 208 weeks before the first week of the benefit
    period referred to in paragraph (2)(a); or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} 260 weeks before the first week of the benefit
    period referred to in paragraph (2)(a).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 12(2) of the Act, the maximum
  number of weeks for which benefits may be paid in a benefit period
  that is established for a claimant who is included in Pilot Project
  No. 21 shall be determined in accordance with the table set out in
  Schedule II.93 by reference to the regional rate of unemployment that
  applies to the claimant and the number of hours of insurable
  employment of the claimant in their qualifying period.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-228, s. 1
- [SOR/2020-111, s. 1](#1247876-1248934){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1247876-1248934.html"}
- [2021, c. 23, s. 350](#1308155-1316939){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1308155-1316939.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-77.992-20200520.html "Link to previous version of section 77.992"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Pilot Project to Provide Additional Weeks of Benefits to Seasonal Workers as a Result of Low Unemployment Rates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1431899 .Subheading}

- **[[77.993]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.993
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 22 is established
  for the purpose of testing whether certain claimants who meet the
  conditions set out in subsection 12(2.3) of the Act require more
  additional weeks of benefits as a result of low unemployment rates.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 22 applies to every claimant whose
  benefit period is established in the period beginning on September 10,
  2023 and ending on September 7, 2024 and who meets the conditions set
  out in subsection 12(2.3) of the Act.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of Pilot Project No. 22, the
  reference to "Schedule V" in the portion of subsection 12(2.3) of the
  Act before paragraph (a) shall be read as a reference to "Schedule
  II.94 of the [Employment Insurance
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332)".

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-272, s. 1](#1429909-1431905){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1429909-1431905.html"}
:::

### [Pilot Project to Provide Additional Hours of Insurable Employment to Claimants in Areas Affected by Natural Disasters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1508593 .Subheading}

- **[[77.994]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.994
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 23 is established
  for the purpose of testing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the effectiveness of a one-time credit of
    additional hours of insurable employment in mitigating the impact of
    evacuations due to natural disasters on the benefits payable to
    claimants under Part I of the Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the effectiveness of using postal codes to identify
    the claimants to whom that credit should apply.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pilot Project No. 23 applies in respect of every
  claimant who, at the time of making an initial claim for benefits
  under Part I of the Act, is ordinarily resident in an area with the
  postal code T0E 1E0, T0E 0C0 or R0B 1C0 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a benefit period beginning no earlier than July 21,
    2024 and no later than the day before the day on which this section
    comes into force is established in respect of the claim and does not
    end in accordance with paragraph 10(8)(d) of the Act before the day
    on which this section comes into force; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claim is made during the period beginning on
    the day on which this section comes into force and ending on July
    19, 2025.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claimant referred to in paragraph (2)(a) is deemed
  to have an additional 300 hours of insurable employment in their
  qualifying period for the benefit period referred to in that
  paragraph.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A claimant referred to in paragraph (2)(b) is deemed
  to have an additional 300 hours of insurable employment in their
  qualifying period in relation to the claim referred to in that
  paragraph unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a benefit period was established for the claimant
    in respect of a previous claim referred to in that paragraph; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant made a previous claim in respect of
    which a benefit period referred to in paragraph (2)(a) was
    established.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The additional hours of insurable employment that a
  claimant is deemed under subsection (3) or (4) to have in their
  qualifying period are also to be considered hours of insurable
  employment for the purposes of subsection 28(4) and paragraphs
  30(1)(a), 31(c) and 32(2)(c) of the Act and subsection 35(3) and
  subparagraph 35(7)(e)(ii) of these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-10, s. 1](#1507195-1508607){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1507195-1508607.html"}
:::

### [Pilot Project Establishing Measures to Respond to Major Changes in Economic Conditions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1524727 .Subheading}

#### [Purpose]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1524728 .Subheading}

**[[77.995]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.995 .sectionLabel}** Pilot Project No.
24 is established for the purpose of testing the outcomes of applying
certain employment insurance measures to respond to the impacts on
employment of major changes in economic conditions.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-115, s. 1](#1524505-1524731){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1524505-1524731.html"}
:::

#### [Waiting Period]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1524732 .Subheading}

**[[77.996]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.996 .sectionLabel}** The Commission
may waive the waiting period in respect of any benefit period that
begins during the period beginning on March 30, 2025 and ending on
October 11, 2025.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-115, s. 1](#1524505-1524735){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1524505-1524735.html"}
:::

#### [Earnings for Benefit Purposes]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1524736 .Subheading}

**[[77.997]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.997 .sectionLabel}** The earnings paid
or payable to a claimant by reason of a lay-off or separation from an
employment do not constitute earnings for the purposes referred to in
subsection 35(2) if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant's benefit period begins during the
  period beginning on March 30, 2025 and ending on October 11, 2025; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} those earnings would, but for this section, be
  allocated under subsections 36(9) to (11) to a number of weeks the
  first week of which falls within the period beginning on March 30,
  2025 and ending on October 11, 2025.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-115, s. 1](#1524505-1524741){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1524505-1524741.html"}
:::

#### [Rate of Unemployment]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1524742 .Subheading}

**[[77.998]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.998 .sectionLabel}** If the later of
the weeks referred to in subsection 10(1) of the Act begins during the
period beginning on April 6, 2025 and ending on October 11, 2025 and the
regional rate of unemployment that applies to the claimant as determined
under section 17 of these Regulations is less than 13.1%, the regional
rate of unemployment that applies to the claimant is deemed to be

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} 7.1%, if the rate determined under section 17 is 6.1%
  or less;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sum of 1% and the rate determined under section
  17, if the latter is greater than 6.1% and less than 12.1%; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} 13.1%, if the rate determined under section 17 is
  12.1% or greater.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-115, s. 1](#1524505-1524748){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1524505-1524748.html"}
- [SOR/2025-150, s. 1](#1534244-1534287){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1534244-1534287.html"}
:::

Version](section-77.998-20250323.html "Link to previous version of section 77.998"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Administrative Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-984399 .Part}

### [Reconsideration under Section 112 of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984400 .Subheading}

- **[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of section 112 of the Act, a request for reconsideration
  of a decision of the Commission must be in writing and contain the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person making the request and their

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} social insurance number or the business number
      assigned to them by the Minister of National Revenue, as the case
      may be,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} address and telephone number, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} facsimile number and email address, if any;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the decision was communicated to
    the person;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the reasons why the person is requesting a
    reconsideration of the decision; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any relevant information that was not previously
    provided to the Commission.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A request for reconsideration must be filed with the
  Commission at the address, facsimile number or email address --- or in
  accordance with the electronic filing procedure --- provided by the
  Commission on the website of the Department of Employment and Social
  Development.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2013, c. 40, s. 237
- SOR/2013-64, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-78-20130401.html "Link to previous version of section 78"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** The Commission must
render decisions on requests for reconsideration without delay.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-64, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-79-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 79"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Suspension of Benefits Pending Appeal]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984416 .Subheading}

**[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** Benefits are not payable
in accordance with a decision of the Employment Insurance Section of the
Social Security Tribunal if, within 21 days after the day on which a
decision is given, the Commission makes an application for leave to
appeal to the Appeal Division of that Tribunal on the ground that the
Employment Insurance Section has erred in law.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-64, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-80-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 80"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** If the Commission makes
an application under the [Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7) for
judicial review of a decision of the Appeal Division of the Social
Security Tribunal, benefits are not payable in respect of the claim for
benefits that is the object of the decision until the final
determination of the application for judicial review.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-64, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-81-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 81"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  decision of the General Division of the Social Security Tribunal that
  declares a provision of the Act or these Regulations to be [ultra
  vires]{.otherLang lang="la"} is appealed by the Commission to the
  Appeal Division of that Tribunal, benefits are not payable in respect
  of the claim for benefits that is the object of the decision --- nor
  in respect of any other claim for benefits made after the decision of
  the General Division, if benefits would not be payable except for that
  decision --- until

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the final determination of the appeal by the Appeal
    Division; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the final determination of any application made by
    the Commission under the [Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7) for
    judicial review of the final determination of the appeal by the
    Appeal Division, if the final determination of the appeal declares
    the provision of the Act or these Regulations to be [ultra
    vires]{.otherLang lang="la"}.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Commission makes an application under the
  [Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7) for judicial review of a decision
  of the Appeal Division of the Social Security Tribunal that declares a
  provision of the Act or these Regulations to be [ultra
  vires]{.otherLang lang="la"}, benefits are not payable in respect of
  the claim for benefits that is the object of the decision --- nor in
  respect of any other claim for benefits made after the decision of the
  Appeal Division, if benefits would not be payable except for that
  decision --- until the final determination of the application for
  judicial review.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-64, s. 3
:::

### [Payment of Benefits Pending a Coverage Decision]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984435 .Subheading}

- **[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), where a
  request is made to an officer of the Canada Customs and Revenue Agency
  under paragraph 90(1)(a), (b), (c) or (d) of the Act for a ruling on
  the question of whether a claimant is or was employed in insurable
  employment for any number of hours in a particular period of
  employment or alleged employment, no benefits are payable in respect
  of any hour that is the subject of the ruling until the later of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the officer\'s ruling on the
    question becomes final; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} where an appeal from the ruling of that officer
      is made to the Minister of National Revenue, the decision of the
      Minister becomes final,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where an appeal from the decision of the
      Minister of National Revenue is filed with the Tax Court of Canada
      pursuant to section 103 of the Act, the decision of the Tax Court
      becomes final,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} where an application is made to the Federal
      Court of Appeal pursuant to section 28 of the [Federal Courts
      Act](/eng/acts/F-7) to review and set aside the decision of the
      Tax Court of Canada, the decision of the Federal Court of Appeal
      becomes final, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} if an application is made to the Supreme Court
      of Canada for leave to appeal from the decision of the Federal
      Court of Appeal, the final decision of the Supreme Court of Canada
      is given.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the claimant
  qualifies to receive benefits under the Act on the basis of hours of
  insurable employment other than the hours that are the subject of the
  application.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 8, s. 182
- SOR/2003-43, s. 4
:::

### [Social Insurance Numbers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984447 .Subheading}

- **[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person employed in insurable employment who is required by section 138
  of the Act to have a Social Insurance Number and who has not yet
  applied to be registered in the register maintained by the Commission
  under subsection 28.1(2) of the [Department of Employment and Social
  Development Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7) shall, within three days after the
  day on which the employment begins, apply to the Commission to be
  registered.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An employer who hires a person in insurable
  employment shall ask the employee, within three days after the day on
  which the employment begins, to inform the employer of their Social
  Insurance Number.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person who becomes employed in insurable
  employment shall, within three days after the day on which the
  employment begins, inform their employer of their Social Insurance
  Number.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} However, a person who becomes employed in insurable
  employment before being registered shall inform their employer of
  their Social Insurance Number within three days after the day on which
  they receive it.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} An employer who is unable to ascertain the Social
  Insurance Number of a person who becomes employed in insurable
  employment shall, within six days after the day on which the
  employment begins, report the matter to the local office of the
  Commission and provide it with the information necessary to identify
  the person.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a person who is employed in insurable
  employment, when the period of their Social Insurance Number validity
  has been extended, a new period of validity has been assigned to their
  number, or a new Social Insurance Number has been assigned to them,
  that person shall inform their employer of that change within three
  days after the day on which they are informed of that change.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-31, s. 22
- SOR/2003-109, s. 1
- 2013, c. 40, s. 236
- SOR/2013-82, s. 17
- SOR/2018-136, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-89-20131212.html "Link to previous version of section 89"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Electronic Systems]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984457 .Subheading}

**[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** Where any information is
provided to the Commission in electronic or other form for the purposes
of the Act or the regulations made under the Act, it shall be provided
in a form and manner approved by the Commission.

- **[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  claimant who makes an initial claim for benefits, or a claim for
  benefits for a week of unemployment, by telephone or other electronic
  means, and provides the information required by section 50 of the Act,
  is deemed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to have expressed an intention to make a claim for
    benefits and to have made such a claim for the purposes of section
    48, 49 or 152.1 of the Act, as the case may be; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to have supplied the information recorded on the
    dated printout from the Commission\'s computerized benefit pay
    system as responses to the questions posed by the interactive
    response system by telephone or other electronic means.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who provides their Social Insurance Number
  and the following information by telephone or other electronic means
  is deemed to have signed their respective claim for benefit:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an initial claim for benefits, their
    date of birth and, if the initial claim is made by electronic means,
    the maiden name of the claimant\'s mother; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claim for benefits for a week of
    unemployment, their personal identification number.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claim for benefits that is made by the means
  referred to in subsection (1) is deemed to have been made on the day
  that the information is received and recorded by the Commission\'s
  computerized benefit pay system.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, sections 38 and 135 of the Act
  apply to a declaration made by electronic means.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The acts and omissions specified in subsections 38(1)
  and 135(1) of the Act are deemed to include the acts and omissions of
  a person who knowingly attempts to interfere with the operation of the
  electronic systems used in the administration of the Act, and the
  penalty provided for by subsection 38(2) of the Act and the punishment
  provided for by subsection 135(3) of the Act are deemed to include the
  right to refuse access to those electronic systems to such a person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-274, s. 4
- SOR/2010-10, s. 29
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-91-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 91"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Direct Deposit of Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984471 .Subheading}

- **[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this section, sections 2 and 4 to 8 of the Direct
  Deposit Regulations apply.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (7), a claimant shall have
  their benefits deposited directly to their bank account if the
  claimant has provided the Commission with the number of an active
  account registered in the claimant\'s name in a financial institution
  in Canada.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claimant is responsible for the accuracy of the
  data supplied by the claimant in order to register for the direct
  deposit of benefits.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} In the absence of any evidence to the contrary, the
  following documents together constitute evidence of the transfer of
  funds to a claimant\'s account and the payment of benefits to the
  claimant:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a document certified by a person acting for the
    Commission to be an extract from the record authorizing a direct
    deposit transaction, in respect of the claimant, to be directed to
    the financial institution where the account of the claimant is
    located; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a certified extract of the records of that
    financial institution indicating the crediting of the amount of the
    deposit to the account of the claimant.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} In order to terminate the direct deposit of benefits,
  the claimant shall notify the Commission.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, sections 38 and 135 of the Act
  apply to declarations made in the context of the payment of benefits
  under this section.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The acts and omissions specified in subsections 38(1)
  and 135(1) of the Act are deemed to include the acts or omissions of a
  person who knowingly attempts to interfere with the operation of the
  electronic systems used for the direct deposit of benefits, and the
  penalty provided for by subsection 38(2) of the Act and the punishment
  provided for by subsection 135(3) of the Act are deemed to include the
  right to refuse access to those electronic systems to such a person.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise advised by the claimant, the
  Commission may redirect the deposit of benefits from an active account
  at a financial institution the number of which was provided to the
  Commission by the claimant to another financial institution, branch or
  active account number when the financial institution notifies the
  Commission of a change of the financial institution, branch or account
  number.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-274, s. 5
:::

## [PART VI]{.HLabel1}[Alternate Access to Special Benefits]{.HTitleText1} {#h-984485 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2000-393, s. 2

\]
:::

- **[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  insured person who does not qualify to receive benefits under section
  7 of the Act and who is claiming special benefits qualifies to receive
  the special benefits if the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has had an interruption of earnings from
    employment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has had 600 or more hours of insurable employment
    in their qualifying period.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 9 of the Act, where an
  insured person who qualifies under subsection (1) to receive special
  benefits makes an initial claim for benefits, a benefit period shall
  be established in respect of the person and, once it is established,
  special benefits are payable to the person in accordance with this
  section for each week of unemployment that falls in the benefit
  period.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), sections 22 to 23.3 of the
  Act apply to the payment of special benefits under this section.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 18 of the Act, a claimant is
  not entitled to be paid special benefits for a working day, in a
  benefit period established under this section, in respect of which the
  claimant fails to prove that on that day the claimant was

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unable to work because of an illness, injury or
    quarantine referred to in subsection 40(4) or (5) and that the
    claimant would otherwise be available for work; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} entitled to benefits under section 22, 23, 23.1,
    23.2 or 23.3 of the Act.

- [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} A claimant to whom benefits are payable under any
  of sections 23 to 23.3 of the Act and whose claim for benefits because
  of illness, injury or quarantine is made for a week that begins on or
  after the day on which this subsection comes into force is not
  disentitled under paragraph (4)(a) for failing to prove that they
  would have been available for work were it not for the illness, injury
  or quarantine.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 18 of the Act, regular benefits
  and additional special benefits are payable for a week of unemployment
  to a claimant who has received special benefits under this section
  where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant has accumulated, since the beginning
    of their benefit period, a number of hours of insurable employment
    that, when added to the number of those hours in their qualifying
    period, equal or exceed the number of hours set out in the table to
    subsection 7(2) of the Act in relation to the regional rate of
    unemployment that was applicable to the claimant at the beginning of
    their benefit period; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any regular benefits or additional special benefits
    are payable to the claimant in that benefit period in accordance
    with the Act, based on the hours of insurable employment in the
    claimant\'s qualifying period.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in this section, the
  provisions of the Act and these Regulations apply to claimants
  claiming benefits under this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-393, s. 3
- SOR/2003-393, s. 14
- SOR/2013-26, s. 3
- SOR/2013-102, s. 19
- SOR/2014-214, s. 1
- SOR/2016-206, s. 9
- SOR/2017-226, s. 19
:::

## [PART VII]{.HLabel1}[Transitional Provisions and Coming into Force]{.HTitleText1} {#h-984504 .Part}

### [Transitional Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984505 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/97-31, s. 23

\]
:::

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** During the period
beginning on June 30, 1996 and ending on January 4, 1997, a reference to
\"hours\" in each of the following provisions shall be read as a
reference to \"weeks\", with such modifications as the circumstances
require:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 35(3);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 35(7)(e);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} subparagraph 37(2)(c)(ii);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} subparagraph 56(2)(b)(iv);

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} section 88; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} subsection 93(5).

**[[94.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94.1 .sectionLabel}** Where, for the
purposes of the Act and in respect of a benefit period established on or
after January 5, 1997, a claimant presents evidence of a week of
insurable employment that occurred before January 1, 1997, that week of
insurable employment shall be considered to represent 35 hours of
insurable employment.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-31, s. 24
:::

**[[94.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94.2 .sectionLabel}** Where, for the
purposes of the Act and in respect of a benefit period established on or
after January 5, 1997, a claimant presents evidence of insurable
employment that occurred before January 1, 1997,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the weeks of insurable employment shall be considered
  to fall consecutively backward beginning on whichever of the following
  days occurred first:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the last day of the last pay period in 1996, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the last day of remunerated insurable employment
    if that day falls before the last pay period in 1996; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the number of weeks of insurable employment is

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 20 or less, the insurable earnings are considered
    to be for that number of weeks, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} greater than 20, the insurable earnings are
    considered to be for the last 20 weeks in the period of employment.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-31, s. 24
:::

**[[94.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94.3 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
providing for the transition from weeks of insurable employment to hours
of insurable employment where the remuneration for the last pay period
beginning in December 1996 is paid to an insured person by their
employer on or after January 1, 1997,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the employer shall assess the insurability of the
  employment of that final pay period under these Regulations as they
  read on January 1, 1997; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant shall, for the purposes of sections 7
  and 12 of the Act, be determined to have worked in insurable
  employment for the greater of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the number of hours provided for by section 94.1,
    and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the number of hours actually worked in each week
    or part of a week of insurable employment, of the final pay period,
    that falls in 1996.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-31, s. 24
:::

**[[94.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94.4 .sectionLabel}** Where, after January
5, 1997, section 59.1 of the Unemployment Insurance Regulations, as it
read on June 29, 1996, applies and where the application of that section
requires the consideration of insured weeks of employment in 1996 or
earlier, section 94.1 of these Regulations shall be applied to convert
those weeks in 1996 or earlier into hours and the number of weeks
stipulated by paragraphs 59.1(2)(a) and (b) of the Unemployment
Insurance Regulations shall be converted into an hourly equivalent by
multiplying the number of weeks required by 35.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-310, s. 8
:::

### [Coming Into Force]{.HTitleText2} {#h-984537 .Subheading}

- **[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  as otherwise provided in this Part, these Regulations come into force
  on June 30, 1996.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 10 to 12 come into force on January 1,
  1997.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 19 to 21 come into force on January 1, 1997.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Sections 22 to 24 and 34, the Table to subsection
  55(7) and paragraph 93(1)(c) come into force on January 5, 1997.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The provision set out in Part I of Schedule III
  applies during the period beginning on June 30, 1996 and ending on
  December 31, 1996.

- [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} The provision set out in Part I.1 of Schedule III
  applies in place of section 12 listed in subsection (1.1) during the
  period beginning on September 12, 1996 and ending on December 31,
  1996.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The provisions set out in Part II of Schedule III
  apply in place of the provisions listed in subsections (2) and (3)
  from June 30, 1996 until the coming into force of those provisions.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-436, s. 1
:::

## [SCHEDULE I]{.scheduleLabel}[(Paragraphs 17(1)(a) and (b), subsections 17.1(1) and 18(1), paragraph 77.2(2)(b), subsections 77.7(2) and 77.8(2), paragraph 77.92(2)(a) and subsections 77.93(2), 77.94(2), 77.95(2) and 77.97(2))]{.OriginatingRef}[Regions for the Purposes of Parts I and VIII of the Act]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-984549 .scheduleLabel}
:::

###### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText} {#interpretation}

- **1** The definitions in this section apply in this Schedule.

  [[Census Agglomeration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Census Agglomeration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} for the purposes of
      sections 2, 5, 7 and 9, means a census agglomeration within the
      meaning of the Statistics Canada document entitled Standard
      Geographical Classification (SGC) 1996 and for the purposes of
      sections 8, 12 and 13, means a census agglomeration within the
      meaning of the Statistics Canada document entitled Standard
      Geographical Classification (SGC) 2011. ([agglomération de
      recensement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Census Division]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Census Division]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a census division
      within the meaning of the Statistics Canada document entitled
      Standard Geographical Classification (SGC) 1996. ([division de
      recensement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Census Metropolitan Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Census Metropolitan Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a census
      metropolitan area within the meaning of the Statistics Canada
      document entitled Standard Geographical Classification (SGC)
      1996. ([région métropolitaine de recensement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Census Subdivision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Census Subdivision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} for the purposes of
      sections 5 and 11, means a census subdivision within the meaning
      of the Statistics Canada document entitled Standard Geographical
      Classification (SGC) 1996 and for the purposes of sections 8 and
      12 to 14, means a census subdivision within the meaning of the
      Statistics Canada document entitled Standard Geographical
      Classification (SGC) 2011. ([subdivision de
      recensement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

###### [Manitoba]{.HTitleText}

- - **6** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Winnipeg, consisting of the
    Census Metropolitan Area of Winnipeg.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of Southern Manitoba, consisting of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} those portions of Census Division Nos. 2, 10, 13
      and 14 that are not part of the Census Metropolitan Area of
      Winnipeg; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 15, 16,
      17 and 20.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} The region of Northern Manitoba, consisting of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 12 that is not
      part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Winnipeg; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 1, 18, 19, 21, 22 and 23.

###### [British Columbia]{.HTitleText}

- - **7** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Southern Interior British
    Columbia, consisting of Census Division Nos. 1, 3, 5, 7, 33, 35, 37
    and 39.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of Vancouver, consisting of the Census
    Metropolitan Area of Vancouver.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} The region of Victoria, consisting of the Census
    Metropolitan Area of Victoria.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} The region of Southern Coastal British Columbia,
    consisting of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 17 that is not
      part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Victoria; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 9 that is not
      part of the Census Agglomeration of Abbotsford; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 19, 21, 23, 25, 27, 29 and
      31.

  - [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Abbotsford, consisting of the
    Census Agglomeration of Abbotsford.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} The region of Northern British Columbia, consisting
    of Census Division Nos. 41, 43, 45, 47, 49, 51, 53, 55, 57 and 59.

###### [Prince Edward Island]{.HTitleText}

- - **8** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Charlottetown, consisting of
    the Census Agglomeration of Charlottetown.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of Prince Edward Island, consisting of
    all Census Subdivisions that are not part of the Census
    Agglomeration of Charlottetown.

###### [Saskatchewan]{.HTitleText}

- - **9** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Regina, consisting of the
    Census Metropolitan Area of Regina.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of Saskatoon, consisting of the Census
    Metropolitan Area of Saskatoon.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} The region of Southern Saskatchewan, consisting of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 6 that is not
      part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Regina;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 11 that is not
      part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Saskatoon;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 12 that is not
      part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Saskatoon and not part of
      the Census Agglomeration of North Battleford; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 1 to 5, 7 to 10 and 13.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} The region of Northern Saskatchewan, consisting of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 12 that is
      part of the Census Agglomeration of North Battleford; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 14 to 18.

###### [Alberta]{.HTitleText}

- - **10** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Calgary, consisting of the
    Census Metropolitan Area of Calgary.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of Edmonton, consisting of the Census
    Metropolitan Area of Edmonton.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} The region of Southern Alberta, consisting of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 6 that is not
      part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Calgary;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} those portions of Census Division Nos. 10 and 11
      that are not part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Edmonton; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 1 to 5, 7, 8, 9, 14, 15, 18
      and 19.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} The region of Northern Alberta, consisting of
    Census Division Nos. 12, 13, 16 and 17.

###### [Newfoundland]{.HTitleText}

- - **11** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of St. John's, consisting of the
    portion of the Census Metropolitan Area of St. John's that is not
    part of Census Subdivision Nos. 01557 and 01559 of Census Division
    No. 1.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of Newfoundland/Labrador, consisting of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Census Subdivision Nos. 01557 and 01559 and the
      portion of Census Division No. 1 that is not part of the Census
      Metropolitan Area of St. John's; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9 and
      10.

###### [Yukon]{.HTitleText}

- - **12** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Whitehorse, consisting of the
    Census Agglomeration of Whitehorse.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of Yukon, consisting of all Census
    Subdivisions that are not part of the Census Agglomeration of
    Whitehorse.

###### [Northwest Territories]{.HTitleText}

- - **13** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Yellowknife, consisting of the
    Census Agglomeration of Yellowknife.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of the Northwest Territories, consisting
    of all Census Subdivisions that are not part of the Census
    Agglomeration of Yellowknife.

###### [Nunavut]{.HTitleText}

- - **14** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Iqaluit, consisting of the
    Census Subdivision No. 6204003.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of Nunavut, consisting of all Census
    Subdivisions except for Census Subdivision No. 6204003.

:::::: section
::::: {#984732 .Schedule}
::: {}
## [SCHEDULE III]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 95)]{.OriginatingRef}[Interim Provisions]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-984733 .scheduleLabel}
:::

###### [PART I]{.HLabel}

- **1** The following is added before section 7:

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - - **6.1** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the employment
      with an employer in any week of a person whose cash earnings are
      less than 20 per cent of the maximum weekly insurable earnings and
      who is employed for less than 15 hours is excluded from insurable
      employment.

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the cash earnings of a person for a pay
      period described in any of the following paragraphs are paid or
      payable to the person otherwise than in respect of weeks,
      subsection (1) does not apply to the employment of that person
      during any such pay period:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a pay period that is a multiple of a week where

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person has cash earnings for each week in
          the pay period, and the cash earnings for the pay period are
          equal to or exceed an amount that is a like multiple of the
          amount of cash earnings referred to in subsection (1), or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person is employed in each week in the
          pay period and the total number of hours of employment in the
          pay period is equal to or exceeds a number of hours that is a
          like multiple of 15;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a pay period that is a semi-monthly pay period
        where

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person has cash earnings for each week or
          part of a week that falls in the pay period, and the cash
          earnings for the pay period are equal to or exceed an amount
          that is 2 1/6 times the amount of cash earnings referred to in
          subsection (1), or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person is employed in each week or part
          of a week that falls in the pay period, and the total number
          of hours of employment in the pay period is 33 hours or more;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a pay period that is a monthly pay period where

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person has cash earnings for each week or
          part of a week that falls in the pay period, and the cash
          earnings for the pay period are equal to or exceed an amount
          that is 4 1/3 times the amount of cash earnings referred to in
          subsection (1), or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person is employed in each week or part
          of a week that falls in the pay period, and the total number
          of hours of employment in the pay period is 65 hours or more;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a pay period of seven consecutive days that
        begins on a day other than Sunday, where

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person has cash earnings for the pay
          period and the cash earnings for the pay period are equal to
          or exceed 20 per cent of the maximum weekly insurable
          earnings, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person is employed in the pay period for
          a total number of hours that is equal to or exceeds 15 hours;
          or

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a pay period that consists of more than one
        period of seven consecutive days and that begins on a day other
        than Sunday, where

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person has cash earnings for each period
          of seven consecutive days in the pay period, and the cash
          earnings for the pay period are equal to or exceed the product
          obtained by multiplying the amount of cash earnings referred
          to in subsection (1) by the number of periods of seven
          consecutive days in the pay period, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person is employed in each period of
          seven consecutive days in the pay period and the total number
          of hours of employment in the pay period is equal to or
          exceeds the product obtained by multiplying 15 by the number
          of periods of seven consecutive days in the pay period.
  :::
  ::::

###### [PART I.1]{.HLabel}

- **1.1** The following applies in place of section 12:

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  ###### [Prescribed Weeks]{.HTitleText}

  - - **12** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph 7(4)(c) of
      the Act, a prescribed week is:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a week in respect of which a claimant has
        received or will receive

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} workers' compensation payments, other than a
          lump sum or pension paid in full and final settlement of a
          claim made for workers' compensation payments,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} under a wage-loss indemnity plan, any
          earnings by reason of illness, injury, quarantine, pregnancy
          or care of a child or children referred to in subsection 23(1)
          of the Act,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} indemnity payments referred to in paragraph
          35(2)(f), or

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} earnings because of which, pursuant to
          section 19 of the Act, no benefits are payable to the
          claimant;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a week in which the claimant was

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} attending a course or program of instruction
          or training to which the claimant was referred by the
          Commission or by an authority designated by the Commission,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} employed under the Self-employment
          employment benefit or the Job Creation Partnerships employment
          benefit established by the Commission under section 59 of the
          Act or under a similar benefit that is provided by a
          provincial government or other organization and is the subject
          of an agreement under section 63 of the Act,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} prevented from establishing an interruption
          of earnings by virtue of the allocation of earnings pursuant
          to section 36,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} serving a week of the waiting period, or

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} disqualified; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a week of unemployment due to a stoppage of
        work attributable to a labour dispute at the factory, workshop
        or other premises at which the claimant was employed.

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), where a week
      is taken into account under any paragraph or subparagraph of that
      subsection, that week may not be taken into account under any
      other paragraph or subparagraph of that subsection.
  :::
  ::::

###### [PART II]{.HLabel}

- **2** The following applies in place of sections 19 to 21:

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  ###### [Information Relating to Employment History]{.HTitleText} {#information-relating-to-employment-history}

  - - **19** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every employer who employs a person in
      insurable employment shall, if that person has an interruption of
      earnings, complete a record of employment in quadruplicate with
      respect to that person on a form supplied by the Commission.

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), copies of the record
      of employment completed pursuant to subsection (1) shall be
      distributed in the following manner:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the copies marked "Employee's copy: Part 1" and
        "Employee's copy: Part 2" shall be delivered or mailed to the
        insured person not later than five days after the later of

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the first day of the interruption of
          earnings, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the employer becomes aware
          of the interruption of earnings;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the copy marked "Commission's copy" shall be
        mailed to the Commission within the time limit set out in
        paragraph (a); and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the copy marked "Employer's copy" shall be kept
        and retained as a part of the employer's records and books of
        account in accordance with subsection 87(3) of the Act.

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, for reasons beyond the employer's control,
      the employer is unable to deliver or mail the copies marked
      "Employee's copy: Part 1" and "Employee's copy: Part 2" of the
      completed record of employment to the insured person within the
      time limit set out in paragraph (2)(a), the employer shall retain
      them until

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} such time as they are requested by the
        Commission or insured person, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 52 weeks after the time limit set out in
        paragraph (2)(a),

      whichever is earlier.

    - [(4)]{.lawlabel} The number of weeks of insurable employment and
      insurable earnings reported in a record of employment with respect
      to an insured person shall be as determined and allocated pursuant
      to Parts III and IV of the Act and any regulations made under
      those Parts, but sections 35 and 36 of these Regulations shall not
      apply in respect of any such determination.

    - [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where the earnings of a claimant for a pay period
      that is current when the claimant has a lay-off or separation from
      employment cannot be ascertained with accuracy, the employer may
      estimate the earnings for that period.

  - - **20** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where an employer has failed to deliver a
      record of employment to an insured person or the Commission
      pursuant to section 19 or the employer is not available or is
      unable to provide information respecting the employment history
      and insurable earnings of a claimant because the employer's
      records are destroyed or lost, a claimant may provide a statement
      containing evidence of their employment history and insurable
      earnings.

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} All the weeks of insurable employment reported in
      a record of employment shall be determined to have occurred
      consecutively and to be weeks that immediately preceded and
      included the week in which the lay-off or separation occurred.

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection 19(4) and section 23,
      for the purposes of Part I of the Act, the number of weeks of
      insurable employment in any one employment period of a claimant
      shall not be greater than the number of weeks or part weeks that
      falls within that employment period.

    - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a bankrupt employer or the trustee of the
      bankrupt employer has not provided the Commission with a record of
      employment for an insured person, the Commission may, in respect
      of that person, determine the number of insurable weeks and the
      amount of insurable earnings for benefit purposes on the basis of
      the payroll and personnel records of the bankrupt employer
      provided to the Commission by the trustee.
  :::
  ::::

- **3** The following applies in place of sections 22 to 24.

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - **22** The earnings to be taken into account for the purpose of
    determining the average weekly insurable earnings for the purposes
    of section 14 of the Act as set out in section 6 of Schedule II to
    the Act are those earnings for which a premium was payable.

  - **23** For the purposes of Part I of the Act, where a claimant's
    earnings were paid or payable in the qualifying period for a period
    otherwise than in respect of weeks (hereinafter called the "pay
    period"), the number of weeks that are to be taken as weeks of
    insurable employment in any one employment period shall

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the pay period consists of seven
      consecutive days and begins on a day other than Sunday and

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the claimant has cash earnings for each pay
        period and the cash earnings for the pay period are equal to or
        exceed 20 per cent of the maximum weekly insurable earnings, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the claimant is employed in each pay period
        for a total number of hours that is equal to or exceeds 15
        hours,

      be equal to the number of pay periods; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the pay period consists of more than one
      period of seven consecutive days and begins on a day other than
      Sunday and

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the claimant has cash earnings for each period
        of seven consecutive days in the pay period, and the cash
        earnings for the pay period are equal to or exceed the product
        obtained by multiplying the amount of cash earnings referred to
        in paragraph (a) by the number of periods of seven consecutive
        days in the pay period, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the claimant is employed in each period of
        seven consecutive days in the pay period, and the total number
        of hours of employment in the pay period is equal to or exceeds
        the product obtained by multiplying 15 by the number of periods
        of seven consecutive days in the pay period,

      be equal to the number of periods of seven consecutive days in the
      pay period.
  :::
  ::::

- **4** The following applies in place of section 34:

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - - **34** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A dependant of a claimant or of the spouse
      of a claimant is a person who is related to the claimant or the
      spouse in the manner described in subsection 251(6) of the [Income
      Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The spouse of a claimant is the person who is
      married to the claimant, and includes a person described in
      subsection 252(4) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph 14(1)(b) of the Act
      as set out in section 6 of Schedule II to the Act, the following
      circumstances are prescribed as circumstances which must exist in
      relation to one or more dependants of a claimant or of the spouse
      of the claimant:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a child tax benefit as provided for in section
        122.61 of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) is payable to
        either the claimant or the spouse during the month in which the
        claimant makes a claim for the benefit rate prescribed by
        paragraph 14(1)(b) of the Act as set out in section 6 of
        Schedule II to the Act; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant or the spouse provides support to
        a dependant of the claimant or of the spouse of the claimant.
  :::
  ::::

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- SOR/2001-495, s. 4

  - [4]{.lawlabel} The exclusion of low-earning weeks from the
    calculation of the rate of weekly benefits payable to a claimant for
    a benefit period established before November 18, 2001 shall be
    calculated in accordance with section 77.1 of the Regulations as it
    read immediately before that date.

- --- SOR/2002-280, s. 2

  - [2]{.lawlabel} These Regulations apply to benefit periods that
    commence on or after the day on which these Regulations come into
    force.

- --- 2009, c. 2, s. 225

  - ###### Subsection 55(7) {#subsection-557 .MarginalNote}

    [225]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks for which benefits may
    be paid to a claimant referred to in subsection 55(7) of the
    [Employment Insurance Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332)
    whose benefit period has not ended before the second Sunday before
    the day on which this Act receives royal assent and does not begin
    after September 11, 2010 is to be determined in accordance with
    Schedule 10.

- --- 2009, c. 2, s. 226

  - ###### Pilot Project No. 10 {#pilot-project-no.-10 .MarginalNote}

    - [226]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Section 77.6 of the [Employment
      Insurance Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) is deemed to
      cease to have effect on the second Saturday before the day on
      which this Act receives royal assent.

    - ###### Transitional {#transitional .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks for which benefits
      may be paid in a benefit period that is established for a claimant
      who is included in Pilot Project No. 10 and whose benefit period
      has not ended before the second Sunday before the day on which
      this Act receives royal assent is to be determined in accordance
      with Schedule I to the [Employment Insurance
      Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted by subsection 224(1).

- --- 2009, c. 30, s. 3

  - ###### Subsection 55(7) of regulations {#subsection-557-of-regulations .MarginalNote}

    [3]{.lawlabel} Despite section 225 of the [Budget Implementation
    Act, 2009](/eng/acts/B-9.858), if a claimant is a claimant referred
    to in subsection 55(7) of the [Employment Insurance
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) who was paid less than 36
    weeks of regular benefits in the 260 weeks before the beginning of
    the claimant's benefit period and that benefit period was
    established during the period that begins on January 4, 2009 and
    ends on June 5, 2010, the number of weeks of benefits in Schedule 10
    to that Act that apply in respect of that claimant is deemed to be
    the number of weeks that would otherwise apply in respect of the
    claimant but for this section increased by

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 5 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least 30%
      of the maximum annual employee's premium in 7 of the 10 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 8 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least 30%
      of the maximum annual employee's premium in 8 of the 11 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 11 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least
      30% of the maximum annual employee's premium in 9 of the 12 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} 14 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least
      30% of the maximum annual employee's premium in 10 of the 13 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} 17 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least
      30% of the maximum annual employee's premium in 11 of the 14 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period; or

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} 20 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least
      30% of the maximum annual employee's premium in at least 12 of the
      15 years before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period.

- --- 2009, c. 30, s. 4

  - ###### Subsection 55(7) of regulations {#subsection-557-of-regulations-1 .MarginalNote}

    [4]{.lawlabel} Despite section 225 of the [Budget Implementation
    Act, 2009](/eng/acts/B-9.858), if a claimant is a claimant referred
    to in subsection 55(7) of the [Employment Insurance
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) who was paid less than 36
    weeks of regular benefits in the 260 weeks before the beginning of
    the claimant's benefit period and that benefit period was
    established during the period that begins on June 6, 2010 and ends
    on July 10, 2010, the number of weeks of benefits in Schedule 10 to
    that Act that apply in respect of that claimant is deemed to be the
    number of weeks that would otherwise apply in respect of the
    claimant but for this section increased by

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 3 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least 30%
      of the maximum annual employee's premium in 8 of the 11 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 6 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least 30%
      of the maximum annual employee's premium in 9 of the 12 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 9 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least 30%
      of the maximum annual employee's premium in 10 of the 13 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} 12 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least
      30% of the maximum annual employee's premium in 11 of the 14 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period; or

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} 15 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least
      30% of the maximum annual employee's premium in at least 12 of the
      15 years before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period.

- --- 2009, c. 30, s. 5

  - ###### Subsection 55(7) of regulations {#subsection-557-of-regulations-2 .MarginalNote}

    [5]{.lawlabel} Despite section 225 of the [Budget Implementation
    Act, 2009](/eng/acts/B-9.858), if a claimant is a claimant referred
    to in subsection 55(7) of the [Employment Insurance
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) who was paid less than 36
    weeks of regular benefits in the 260 weeks before the beginning of
    the claimant's benefit period and that benefit period was
    established during the period that begins on July 11, 2010 and ends
    on August 7, 2010, the number of weeks of benefits in Schedule 10 to
    that Act that apply in respect of that claimant is deemed to be the
    number of weeks that would otherwise apply in respect of the
    claimant but for this section increased by

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 1 week, if the claimant contributed at least 30%
      of the maximum annual employee's premium in 9 of the 12 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 4 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least 30%
      of the maximum annual employee's premium in 10 of the 13 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 7 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least 30%
      of the maximum annual employee's premium in 11 of the 14 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period; or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} 10 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least
      30% of the maximum annual employee's premium in at least 12 of the
      15 years before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period.

- --- 2009, c. 30, s. 6

  - ###### Subsection 55(7) of regulations {#subsection-557-of-regulations-3 .MarginalNote}

    [6]{.lawlabel} Despite section 225 of the [Budget Implementation
    Act, 2009](/eng/acts/B-9.858), if a claimant is a claimant referred
    to in subsection 55(7) of the [Employment Insurance
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) who was paid less than 36
    weeks of regular benefits in the 260 weeks before the beginning of
    the claimant's benefit period and that benefit period was
    established during the period that begins on August 8, 2010 and ends
    on September 11, 2010, the number of weeks of benefits in Schedule
    10 to that Act that apply in respect of that claimant is deemed to
    be the number of weeks that would otherwise apply in respect of the
    claimant but for this section increased by

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 2 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least 30%
      of the maximum annual employee's premium in 11 of the 14 years
      before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 5 weeks, if the claimant contributed at least 30%
      of the maximum annual employee's premium in at least 12 of the 15
      years before the beginning of the claimant's benefit period.

- --- 2009, c. 30, s. 7

  - ###### Subsection 55(10) of regulations {#subsection-5510-of-regulations .MarginalNote}

    [7]{.lawlabel} With respect to a claimant to whom any of sections 3
    to 6 applies whose benefit period has not been extended under any of
    subsections 10(13) to (13.3) of the [Employment Insurance
    Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), subsection 55(10) of the [Employment
    Insurance Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) is deemed to
    read as follows:

    > ::: ReadAsText
    > - [(10)]{.lawlabel} In a claimant's benefit period, a claim- ant
    >   who is not in Canada or a claimant referred to in subsection
    >   (8), subject to the applicable maximums set out in paragraphs
    >   (7)(a) and (b), may combine the weeks of benefits to which the
    >   claimant is entitled, but the total number of weeks of benefits
    >   shall not exceed 50 or, if the maximum number of weeks during
    >   which benefits may be paid to a claimant under section 225 of
    >   the [Budget Implementation Act, 2009](/eng/acts/B-9.858) is
    >   equal to or greater than 50 weeks as a result of the application
    >   of any provision of any Act of Parliament, other than the
    >   [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), the number that
    >   corresponds to that maximum number of weeks.
    > :::

- --- SOR/2012-260, s. 4

  - - [4]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 55(4) of the
      [Employment Insurance Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332),
      as enacted by section 1, does not apply in respect of a claimant
      referred to in that subsection whose benefit period was
      established before December 9, 2012.

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 55(4) of the [Employment Insurance
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332), as it read immediately
      before December 9, 2012, continues to apply in respect of a
      claimant referred to in that subsection whose benefit period was
      established before that day.

- --- SOR/2012-260, s. 5

  - - [5]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 55.01(3) of the
      [Employment Insurance Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332),
      as enacted by section 2, does not apply in respect of a
      self-employed person referred to in that subsection whose benefit
      period was established before December 9, 2012.

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 55.01(3) of the [Employment Insurance
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332), as it read immediately
      before December 9, 2012, continues to apply in respect of a
      self-employed person referred to in that subsection whose benefit
      period was established before that day.

- --- 2022, c. 10, s. 350.1

  - ###### Continued application --- before September 25, 2022 {#continued-application-before-september-25-2022 .MarginalNote}

    - [350.1]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 35(6), paragraph
      35(7)(g) and section 36 of the former Regulations continue to
      apply in respect of a claimant's earnings if, but for this
      subsection, the earnings would be allocated under subsection 36(9)
      or (10) of the new Regulations to a number of weeks the first week
      of which falls within the period beginning on September 26, 2021
      and ending on September 24, 2022.

    - ###### Definitions {#definitions .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in this section.

      [[claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

      :   [[claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
          subsection 2(1) of the [Employment Insurance
          Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6). ([prestataire]{.DefinedTermLink
          lang="fr"})

      [[earnings]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

      :   [[earnings]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the earnings referred
          to in subsections 36(9) and (10) of the new
          Regulations. ([rémunération]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

      [[former Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

      :   [[former Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
          [Employment Insurance
          Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) as they read
          immediately before September 25, 2022. ([ancien
          règlement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

      [[new Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

      :   [[new Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Employment
          Insurance Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) as they
          read on September 25, 2022. ([nouveau
          règlement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

## AMENDMENTS NOT IN FORCE {#amendments-not-in-force .scheduleTitleText}

- --- SOR/2025-74, s. 38

  - [38]{.lawlabel} Section 80 of the [Employment Insurance
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332)[[Footnote
    ]{.wb-invisible}^1^](#fn_81000-2-5492_hq_1724685825){#fn_81000-2-5492_hq_1724685825-ID0EBCADAA}
    is replaced by the following:

    - ::: Footnote
      [[Return to footnote
      ]{.wb-invisible}^1^](#fn_81000-2-5492_hq_1724685825-ID0EBCADAA){#fn_81000-2-5492_hq_1724685825}SOR/96-332
      :::

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - [80]{.lawlabel} Benefits are not payable in accordance with a
      decision of the Employment Insurance Section of the Social
      Security Tribunal or the Employment Insurance Board of Appeal
      established under section 43.01 of the [Department of Employment
      and Social Development Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7) if, within 21 days
      after the day on which a decision is given, the Commission appeals
      to the Appeal Division of the Social Security Tribunal on the
      ground that the Employment Insurance Section or the Board of
      Appeal, as the case may be, has erred in law.
    :::
    ::::

- --- SOR/2025-74, s. 39

  - [39]{.lawlabel} Section 80 of the Regulations is replaced by the
    following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText

      decision of the Employment Insurance Board of Appeal established
      under section 43.01 of the [Department of Employment and Social
      Development Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7) if, within 21 days after the day
      on which a decision is given, the Commission appeals to the Appeal
      Division of the Social Security Tribunal on the ground that the
      Board of Appeal has erred in law.
    :::
    ::::

- --- SOR/2025-74, s. 40

  - - [40]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The portion of subsection 82(1)
      of the Regulations before paragraph (a) is replaced by the
      following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - - [82]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} If either a decision of the
          General Division of the Social Security Tribunal that declares
          a provision of the Act or these Regulations to be [ultra
          vires]{.otherLang lang="la"} or a decision of the Employment
          Insurance Board of Appeal established under section 43.01 of
          the [Department of Employment and Social Development
          Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7) that declares a provision of these
          Regulations to be [ultra vires]{.otherLang lang="la"} is
          appealed by the Commission to the Appeal Division of the
          Social Security Tribunal, benefits are not payable in respect
          of the claim for benefits that is the object of the decision
          --- nor in respect of any other claim for benefits made after
          that decision, if benefits would not be payable except for
          that decision --- until
      :::
      ::::

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 82(1)(b) of the French version of the
      Regulations is replaced by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: {.AmendedText lang="fr"}
      - [b)]{.lawlabel} une décision définitive n'a pas été rendue à
        l'égard de la demande de contrôle judiciaire, présentée par la
        Commission en vertu de la [Loi sur les Cours
        fédérales](/eng/acts/F-7), à l'égard de la décision définitive
        rendue dans l'appel par la division d'appel, si celle-ci déclare
        invalide la disposition de la Loi ou du présent règlement.
      :::
      ::::

- --- SOR/2025-74, s. 41

  - - [41]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The portion of subsection 82(1)
      of the Regulations before paragraph (a) is replaced by the
      following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - - [82]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a decision of the
          Employment Insurance Board of Appeal established under section
          43.01 of the [Department of Employment and Social Development
          Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7) that declares a provision of these
          Regulations to be [ultra vires]{.otherLang lang="la"} is
          appealed by the Commission to the Appeal Division of the
          Social Security Tribunal, benefits are not payable in respect
          of the claim for benefits that is the object of the decision
          --- nor in respect of any other claim for benefits made after
          that decision, if benefits would not be payable except for
          that decision --- until
      :::
      ::::

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 82(1)(b) of the Regulations is replaced
      by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the final determination of any application made
        by the Commission under the [Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7)
        for judicial review of the final determination of the appeal by
        the Appeal Division, if the final determination of the appeal
        declares the provision of these Regulations to be [ultra
        vires]{.otherLang lang="la"}.
      :::
      ::::
